### [1] [LEDGER: Claim-to-Evidence Trace Graphs for Auditing LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18398)
*Daehong Kim,Haichao Miao,Shusen Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出LEDGER系统，通过构建分层追踪图来改进LLM代理长时工作流程的审计能力，将原始执行事件组织为证据节点和工作流节点，并添加语义边以支持证据为中心的审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理能够快速执行复杂的长时技术工作流程，生产力瓶颈已从产出转向审计。现有可观测性系统虽能展示细粒度执行事件，但审查者仍需手动重建哪些操作、工件和验证步骤对特定结论重要，缺乏结构化的证据关联视图。

Method: LEDGER构建分层的追踪图：保留追踪记录（Trace Records），将其分组为证据节点（Evidence Nodes）和工作流节点（Workflow Nodes），将工件表示为证据锚点，并添加类型化的语义边来连接主张（claims）与支持性操作、工件和检查。

Result: 通过数据分析和编码示例，展示了LEDGER生成的追踪图能够揭示工作流决策、工件谱系、修复步骤、验证覆盖率和主张支持路径，从而实现证据中心的审计。

Conclusion: LEDGER通过分层证据图结构有效解决了LLM代理输出的可审计性和可信性问题，将审计从"可见性"提升到"可理解性"和"可验证性"层面。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now carry out long-horizon technical workflows involving complex tool use, code execution, file edits, and generated artifacts. As agents do more work faster, the productivity bottleneck shifts from producing outputs to auditing whether those outputs are correct and trustworthy. Agent observability systems make fine-grained execution events visible, but visibility alone still leaves reviewers to reconstruct which actions, artifacts, and validation steps matter for a particular conclusion. We introduce LEDGER - Layered Evidence and Decision Graphs for Execution Review, a tracing and review system that builds layered trace graphs over observed agent sessions. LEDGER preserves Trace Records while grouping them into Evidence Nodes and Workflow Nodes, representing artifacts as evidence anchors, and adding typed semantic edges that connect claims to supporting actions, artifacts, and checks. Through data-analysis and coding examples, we show how the resulting traces expose workflow decisions, artifact lineage, repair steps, validation coverage, and claim-support paths for evidence-centered audit.

</details>


### [2] [Multimodal Rapport Estimation in Real-World HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18401)
*Akihiro Sakuramoto,Takato Hayashi,Ryo Miyoshi,Yuki Okafuji,Shogo Okada*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于日本药妆店中62次多模态真实人机交互会话，考察了第三方评分的亲和度（rapport）自动估计。结果表明零样本大语言模型在真实场景中表现强劲，音频和视觉模型提供互补信息，其中Gemini 2.5 Flash单一模型性能突出，结合Gemini（文本）、HuBERT和V-JEPA的融合模型整体最优。估计性能在不同交互时长和群体规模条件下存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境下的人机交互质量评估是重要挑战，但现有自动评估方法多基于受控实验室环境开发，能否直接应用于用户可自由退出、多参与者自然出现的真实世界场景尚不明确。

Method: 使用62个在日本药妆店采集的多模态录音会话，比较零样本LLM、预训练的文本/音频/视觉模型及其预测级融合方法，对第三方评分的亲和度分数进行自动估计。

Result: 零样本LLM在真实HRI中表现强劲，音频和视觉模型提供互补信息；Gemini 2.5 Flash作为单一模型表现最佳，Gemini（文本）+HuBERT+V-JEPA的融合模型总体最优；估计性能随交互时长和群体规模条件而变化。

Conclusion: 真实世界HRI中的亲和度估计需要考虑实验室设置之外的情境变异性，评估方法和模型设计应适应这些现实条件的差异。

Abstract: Evaluating interaction quality in real-world HRI is an important challenge. If interaction quality can be estimated reliably, the results can be used to improve dialogue strategies and ultimately enable robots to adapt their behavior autonomously. However, existing automatic evaluation methods have been developed primarily in controlled laboratory settings, and it remains unclear whether they can be directly applied to real-world environments, where users are free to disengage and multi-party participation may arise naturally. In this study, we investigate the automatic estimation of third-party-rated rapport scores using 62 sessions of multimodal recordings collected in a Japanese drugstore. We compare zero-shot LLMs, pretrained text, audio, and visual models, and their prediction-level fusion. The results show that, in real-world HRI, zero-shot LLMs achieve strong performance, while audio and visual models tend to provide complementary information. In particular, Gemini 2.5 Flash performs strongly as a single model, and a fusion model combining Gemini (text) with HuBERT and V-JEPA performs best overall. Further analyses showed that estimation performance varied across interaction-duration and group-size conditions. These findings suggest that rapport estimation in real-world HRI requires evaluation and model design that account for contextual variability beyond that assumed in laboratory settings.

</details>


### [3] [Reducing Technician Search Burden: A Multimodal RAG for Cessna 172 Maintenance Manual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18465)
*Seongjun Ha,Md Rashedul Islam,Gaurav Nanda,Damon Lercel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究针对Cessna 172维修手册开发了一种多模态手册检索器（MMR）及多模态RAG（MRAG）流水线，实现93.37%的recall@5检索准确率和87.20%的语义相似度，平均每次查询成本仅$0.0091，可显著减少技术人员查阅手册的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机维修手册对正确维护至关重要，但技术人员常因手册导航困难且耗时不愿查阅。现有RAG模型仅支持文本检索，无法利用手册中丰富的过程图、警示信息等视觉内容，因此需要开发多模态检索方案。

Method: 以Cessna 172维修手册为研究对象，构建多模态手册检索器（MMR）实现手册页面的多模态检索；在此基础上构建多模态RAG（MRAG）流水线，将检索到的页面输入视觉语言模型生成回答；使用涵盖程序、图表、警示/安全信息和规格的四类合成查询评估性能，并从推理时间、运营成本和可解释性三个维度进行实用性评估。

Result: MMR实现93.37%的recall@5；MRAG回答的语义相似度达87.20%；平均检索5页耗时11.93秒，响应生成4.95秒，单次查询成本$0.0091；通过热力图可视化验证了可解释性。

Conclusion: 针对C172-MM的MRAG流水线能够有效减少技术人员搜索手册和检索多模态信息的时间，为通用航空维修领域提供了实用的智能化辅助方案。

Abstract: Proper use of the aircraft maintenance manual is essential for correct maintenance, providing procedures, diagrams, cautions, and specifications. However, technicians often avoid consulting it because it is difficult to navigate and time-consuming under strict schedules. Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) models have recently been introduced in aircraft maintenance, yet existing models focus solely on textual retrieval. This research therefore targeted the Cessna 172 Maintenance Manual (C172-MM), widely used in general aviation, and developed a multimodal manual retriever (MMR) capable of retrieving multimodal manual pages. Retrieval performance was evaluated using synthetic queries covering procedures, diagrams, caution/safety information, and specifications; the MMR achieved 93.37% recall@5. Beyond retrieval, a multimodal RAG (MRAG) pipeline was examined, in which retrieved pages were input to a vision-language model that generated responses to the synthetic queries, achieving 87.20% semantic similarity to ground-truth answers. Three practical feasibilities were also assessed: inference time, operational cost, and interpretability. Average retrieval time for five pages was 11.93 seconds and response generation took 4.95 seconds, at $0.0091 per query, while interpretability was validated through heatmap visualizations. These results indicate that the MRAG pipeline for the C172-MM can reduce the time technicians spend searching manuals and retrieving multimodal information.

</details>


### [4] [Designing Social Robots for Social-Cognition Training with Autistic Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18488)
*Yuval Zohar,Mordi Benhamou,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过在线焦点小组与协同设计工作坊，邀请五名自闭症成人共同探讨社交机器人用于社交认知训练应具备的特质，提炼出七项核心设计要求，指出自闭症成人期望机器人作为私密、可配置的排练伙伴而非陪伴者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交机器人的自闭症干预研究过度聚焦于儿童群体，且鲜少让自闭症成人成为设计的主动贡献者，导致系统设计与成人日常生活中真实的社交认知挑战之间存在明显错配。

Method: 招募五名自闭症成人，开展90分钟在线焦点小组与协同设计工作坊，采用开放式讨论与共享数字白板上的结构化协同设计活动相结合的方式，对言语与视觉数据进行反思性主题分析。

Result: 分析得出七个核心设计需求主题：机器人应作为脚手架而非替代品、优先真实性而非舒适度、提供个性化且用户可控的反馈、适应情绪自我意识缺口、尊重隐私与情境边界、支持真实社交情境的排练、以及身份/形态/表达可配置。

Conclusion: 自闭症成人设想中的社交机器人并非陪伴者或实时社交助手，而是私密的、可配置的排练伙伴，其目标在于长期支持独立性的发展。

Abstract: Social robots have been widely explored as tools for autism intervention, yet this literature has focused predominantly on children and has rarely involved autistic adults as active contributors to design. This creates a mismatch between existing systems and the social-cognitive challenges autistic adults actually face in everyday life, including navigating ambiguous interpersonal contexts, managing conversational timing, and interpreting implied emotional meaning. To address this gap, we conducted an online focus group and co-design session with five autistic adults to explore what a social robot for social-cognition training should do, how it should interact, and under what conditions it would be genuinely useful. The 90-minute session combined open discussion with structured co-design activities on a shared digital whiteboard, and the resulting verbal and visual data were analysed using reflexive thematic analysis. The analysis yielded seven themes that define core design requirements: the robot should function as a scaffold rather than a substitute, prioritise authenticity over comfort, provide personalised and user-controlled feedback, accommodate emotional self-awareness gaps, respect privacy and contextual boundaries, support rehearsal for real-world social situations, and remain configurable in identity, form, and expression. Together, the findings suggest that autistic adults envision the robot not as a companion or live social assistant, but as a private, configurable rehearsal partner designed to support independence over time.

</details>


### [5] [Measuring Proof Burden in Public Bounty Listings: A RentAHuman Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18547)
*Iman YeckehZaare*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文针对在线赏金市场中的"证明负担"（Proof Burden）进行了描述性研究。作者手动审计了RentAHuman和Human Pages两个市场2026年5月31日的981条列表快照，通过13个特征（11种证据类型、重复监测、物理世界行动）和0-5分的证明负担评分，发现56.2%的任务评分达到4或5分，涵盖154种不同的特征组合。研究发现agent/机器人标注的列表更可能包含物理行动、位置证明或重复监测要求，但作者明确指出这是后验假设而非确认性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线赏金市场允许请求者发布付费任务，但工人不仅需要完成任务，还可能需要提供证明，而证明可能涉及暴露身份、位置、使用个人账户、公开发布、物理世界行动或后续重复验证等要求——这些"证明负担"均未在标价中披露。作者旨在系统性地测量和描述这些隐藏的广告要求，而非工人提交的内容或经历。

Method: 作者对2026年5月31日的快照进行了人工审计，覆盖RentAHuman和Human Pages两个市场共981条列表。两名独立编码者记录了13个特征（11种证据类型、重复监测、物理世界行动）并打分0-5的证明负担评分，由第三名盲审者解决所有分歧。经过计划内容筛选后，主分析人群为779条赏金/任务列表。对标记为agent或bot的请求者账户与人类标记账户进行了探索性比较，并明确指出该比较为事后选择。

Result: 在779条主要分析列表中，438条（56.2%）得分为4或5，共出现154种不同的特征组合。agent或bot标注的列表中有75.0%包含物理世界行动、位置证明或重复监测，而人类标注的列表为55.3%；但4-5分高评分占比并无明显差异。由于标签为自报或平台分配、agent/bot列表仅来自20个显示名称、且比较为事后选择，该差异仅作为假设而非确认性结论。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了13项要求的词汇表、经过裁决的人工审计数据集和描述性案例研究。证明负担评分仅作为次要筛选摘要。研究尚未提供经工人验证的度量工具或自动化检测器，未来需要进一步验证和工具开发。

Abstract: Online bounty markets let requesters advertise paid tasks. Workers may be asked not just to complete a task but to prove it, and proof can mean exposure: revealing identity or location, using a personal account, posting publicly, acting in the physical world, or repeated evidence at later checks, none disclosed by the posted price. We call these advertised requirements proof burden and measure them on RentAHuman, a 2026 market publicized as a place for AI agents to hire humans. We study what listings request, not what workers submit or experience.
  We manually audited a nonrandom May 31, 2026 snapshot: every listing our searches returned from RentAHuman and Human Pages, another such market (981 listings, all but one from RentAHuman). Two independent coders recorded 13 features (11 kinds of evidence, recurring monitoring, physical-world action) and our 0-5 Proof Burden Score; a blinded third resolved all disagreements. A planned content screen leaves 779 bounty/task listings as the primary population; 438 (56.2%) score 4 or 5, spanning 154 distinct feature combinations: a checklist, not a single score, tells workers what a listing entails.
  Platform metadata labels some requester accounts as agents or bots. Exploratory comparisons show physical-world action, location proof, or recurring monitoring in 75.0% of agent-or-bot-labeled versus 55.3% of human-labeled listings, though score-4-or-5 shares did not clearly differ. The labels are self-reported or platform-assigned, the agent-or-bot-labeled listings come from only 20 displayed names, and the comparison was chosen post hoc, after seeing the data: a hypothesis, not a confirmed difference. We contribute the 13-requirement vocabulary, the adjudicated manual audit, and this descriptive case study; the score is a secondary screening summary. The study offers no worker-validated measure or automated detector yet.

</details>


### [6] [SemanticSlider3D: Training-Free Continuous Semantic Editing for 3D Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18560)
*Ru Wang,Rahul Jain,Koichiro Niinuma,Aakar Gupta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出SemanticSlider3D，一种无需逐属性训练即可对3D物体进行连续语义属性编辑的技术，利用3D生成模型的潜在空间构建语义编辑方向，生成多样且连贯的3D变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D内容创作中对连续语义属性的细粒度控制至关重要，但传统3D建模工作流和基于提示的生成AI交互方式支持不足。2D图像生成中滑块方法已被证明有效，但3D领域尚无对应方法，且由于几何完整性和跨视角一致性等3D特有挑战，直接扩展2D方法并不可行。

Method: SemanticSlider3D通过在先进的3D生成模型的潜在空间中构建语义编辑方向来实现连续属性编辑，无需针对每个属性进行额外训练。用户指定属性后，管线自动构建编辑方向，呈现多样且连贯的3D变体频谱。

Result: 在50个3D物体-属性对数据集上的技术验证中，五名评估者一致认为该方法在变化范围、一致性、3D物体质量和属性解耦方面优于基线（2D滑块结合图像转3D模型）。六名参与者的探索性研究显示，该方法支持3D原型设计中的决策，并被视为现有工作流的有价值补充。

Conclusion: SemanticSlider3D为3D物体提供了类似2D滑块方法的细粒度语义控制能力，无需逐属性训练，且在质量和一致性上超越基线，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Fine-grained control over continuous semantic attributes of 3D objects is essential for 3D content creation, but is not well supported by conventional 3D modeling workflows or prompt-based interaction with existing generative AI tools. While slider-based methods have proven effective for fine-grained semantic control in 2D image generation, no equivalent approach exists for 3D. Extending these 2D methods to 3D is non-trivial due to challenges unique to 3D, including geometric integrity and cross-view coherence. We present SemanticSlider3D, a technique for continuous semantic attribute editing of 3D objects that requires no per-attribute training. Given a user-specified attribute, our pipeline constructs a semantic editing direction in the latent space of a state-of-the-art 3D generation model, presenting a diverse and coherent spectrum of 3D variations. A technical validation on a dataset of 50 3D object-attribute pairs shows our method was preferred by all five human assessors across variation range, consistency, 3D object quality, and attribute disentanglement, over a baseline combining a 2D slider with an image-to-3D model. An exploratory study with six participants demonstrates that SemanticSlider3D supported decision-making in 3D prototyping and was perceived as a valuable addition to existing workflows.

</details>


### [7] [Report on The 1st Workshop on Human-Centered Proactive and Personalized Agents for Interactive Information Access at CHIIR 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18638)
*Kirandeep Kaur,Vinayak Gupta,Tanya Roosta,Madhura Raju,Grace Hui Yang,Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文总结了第一届"以人为中心的前摄性与个性化智能体交互信息访问研讨会"（HLPPA）的报告，该研讨会汇集了信息检索、人机交互、对话系统、AI伦理、认知科学等多个领域的跨学科讨论，围绕前摄性智能体的设计挑战展开，强调前摄性应被视为一种需要恰当时机、透明、可质疑且与用户目标一致的主动性行为，而非仅仅是更早的行动或更好的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式信息访问正从被动的查询-响应模式转向智能体系统，实现个性化交互、上下文记忆、潜在需求推断、下一步推荐和主动支持。这一转变在带来机会的同时也引发了关于自主性、隐私、信任、透明度、用户福祉和评估等关键问题，需要一个跨学科论坛来系统审视这些问题。

Method: 通过组织跨学科研讨会，采用特邀报告、论文报告和开放式讨论等形式，围绕校准主动权、知识缺口导航、长期记忆、价值敏感设计、隐式个性化、AI介导关怀、前摄性对话和超越任务准确性的评估等主题展开深入研讨。

Result: 研讨会形成了核心共识：前摄性不应仅被理解为更早的行动或改进的预测，而应视为一种主动性形式，必须恰当计时、保持透明、允许质疑，并与用户目标保持一致。同时识别出了前摄性个性化智能体在设计中的关键研究挑战。

Conclusion: 为前摄性和个性化智能体在交互式信息访问中的设计提供了综合的研究挑战总结，强调了以人为中心的设计原则在前摄性AI系统中的核心地位。

Abstract: Interactive information access is increasingly moving beyond reactive query-response paradigms toward agentic systems that can personalize interaction, retain context, infer latent needs, recommend next steps, and initiate support. This shift creates new opportunities for adaptive and context-aware assistance, while also raising important questions about autonomy, privacy, trust, transparency, user welfare, and evaluation. The First Workshop on Human-Centered Proactive and Personalized Agents for Interactive Information Access provided an interdisciplinary forum for examining these questions across information retrieval, human-computer interaction, dialogue systems, AI ethics, cognitive science, learning technologies, and human-centered AI. Through invited talks, paper presentations, and open discussion, the workshop engaged with topics including calibrated initiative, knowledge-gap navigation, long-term memory, value-sensitive design, implicit personalization, AI-mediated care, proactive dialogue, and evaluation beyond task accuracy. A central theme across the workshop was that proactivity should not be understood only as earlier action or improved prediction, but as a form of initiative that must be appropriately timed, transparent, contestable, and aligned with user goals. This report summarizes the workshop and synthesizes the research challenges it surfaced for designing proactive and personalized agents in interactive information access.

</details>


### [8] [Sounds Uncertain: Exploring the Affective Aspects of Sonification for Uncertainty Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18680)
*Marcel-Simon Dutt,Sita A. Vriend,Elias Elmquist,Daniel Weiskopf*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过共同设计研究探讨了声音化如何在不确定性可视化中传达情感。研究发现不确定性通常与波浪状、不祥的听觉特质相关，而中立性则与清晰、放松的听觉特质相关，为集成声音化以传达不确定性情感成分的可视化设计提供了见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然声音在媒体中被广泛用于唤起情感，但关于声音化如何支持情感可视化（尤其是不确定性可视化中的情感传达）的研究还很有限。

Method: 采用共同设计（co-design）研究，让参与者创建两种声音化效果来伴随可视化：一种传达不确定性的情感成分，另一种传达中立性。

Result: 不确定性通常与波浪状听觉特质和 ominous（不祥）情绪相关；中立性则与清晰、放松的听觉特质相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计集成声音化以传达不确定性情感成分的可视化提供了设计启示和见解。

Abstract: Affective visualization can influence how users perceive, interpret, and engage with data by embedding and conveying emotion through visual design. While sound is widely used in media to evoke emotions, little is known about how sonification can support affective visualization. In this work, we investigate how sonification can communicate emotion in uncertainty visualizations through a co-design study. Participants created two sonifications to accompany a visualization: one conveying the affective component of uncertainty and one conveying neutrality. Our findings show that uncertainty was commonly associated with wavy auditory qualities related to an ominous sentiment. On the other hand, neutrality was associated with clear and relaxing auditory qualities. These results provide insights for the design of visualizations that integrate sonification to communicate the affective component of uncertainty.

</details>


### [9] [Model Literacy: An Extra Summative Evaluation Factor for Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18721)

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出"模型素养"（即用户对VA技术中所用分析模型的了解程度）作为影响可视化分析性能的新因素。通过PCA和t-SNE两种降维模型的受控实验，发现模型任务准确率与VA任务准确率呈正相关，且PCA比t-SNE更强；完成时间上没有稳定的效率增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VA评估方法多借鉴信息可视化的总结性评估方法，但VA流程中新增的数据分析/建模阶段带来了额外挑战。受到可视化素养概念的启发，作者希望探究"模型素养"作为影响VA性能的额外因素。

Method: 开展受控实验，以多维数据的可视分析为任务，使用两种常见降维模型（PCA和t-SNE）进行对照实验，测量模型任务准确率与VA任务准确率之间的相关性，并分析完成时间数据。

Result: 模型任务准确率与VA任务准确率呈正相关；PCA的相关性比t-SNE更强；完成时间数据未显示稳定的效率增益，表明模型直观性差异可能解释何时模型知识能缩短任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 模型素养是影响VA性能的重要因素，其影响程度可能与模型输出的可读性相关。该发现可丰富VA评估方法，并为开发更严谨的模型素养评估工具提供方向。

Abstract: Understanding and enhancing visual analytics (VA) performance is important for maximizing their impact. Existing studies have successfully applied well-established summative evaluation methods from information visualization to the VA context, yet the recent emphasis on an extra data analysis/modeling stage in the VA pipeline poses an additional challenge. Inspired by the modern concept of visualization literacy, this paper examines model literacy, namely users' knowledge of the analysis model used in a VA technique, as an additional factor for VA performance. Results from a controlled study on the visual analysis of multidimensional data with two dimensionality-reduction models indicate a positive correlation between model-task accuracy and VA-task accuracy. The study involves two common dimensionality-reduction models, PCA and t-SNE. The correlation is stronger for PCA than for t-SNE in the current task design, a pattern consistent with the possibility that VA effectiveness is more closely associated with model literacy when model outputs are less directly readable from the visualization. Completion-time evidence does not show a stable efficiency gain, suggesting that differences in model intuitiveness may help explain when model knowledge shortens task completion and when it involves additional interpretive effort. The findings of this study suggest ways to further enrich VA evaluation methods and provide directions for developing more rigorous model-literacy assessment instruments.

</details>


### [10] [Engineering Psychological Safety in Autonomous Vehicles: A Systems-Theoretic Framework for Psychological Safety in Autonomous Vehicles and its Validation in Real-World Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18778)
*Yandika Sirgabsou,Benjamin Hardin,François Leblanc,Efi Raili,David Jackson,Pericle Salvini,Lars Kunze,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个基于系统理论的心理安全评估框架（AV-PsySafe），将STAMP模型扩展至涵盖信任、感知控制、可预测性和感知支持等心理构念，并引入心理安全完整性等级（PsySIL）来系统识别、评估和缓解自动驾驶车辆中的心理风险，通过实际场景验证证明了其实践可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的社会可接受性受限于超出传统物理安全范畴的心理障碍，现有研究虽已识别信任和感知安全等影响因素，但缺乏系统化的机制和工程方法来识别、评估和缓解人机交互产生的心理风险。

Method: 首先将系统理论的事故模型和过程（STAMP）扩展至包含信任、感知控制、可预测性和感知支持等关键心理构念，构建全面的心理安全风险模型；随后开发危险分析方法AV-PsySafe，用于系统识别心理危害、不安全控制行为和损失场景，并引入心理安全完整性等级（PsySIL）支持风险优先级排序；最后通过方法论指南、标准化分析模板和分析师反馈收集等方式在真实自动驾驶场景中进行结构化验证。

Result: 验证结果表明该框架能够被实践者一致地应用，并产生对心理风险有意义且深入的洞见，证明了其实际可用性和相关性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了在自动驾驶系统中协同评估心理安全与物理安全的理论基础和实际可行性，为推动更以人为中心、更值得信赖的自动驾驶技术开发做出贡献。

Abstract: Despite rapid technological advances, the societal acceptability of autonomous vehicles (AVs) remains limited by psychological barriers that extend beyond traditional concerns of physical safety. While factors such as trust and perceived safety are known to influence user acceptance, there is a lack of formalized mechanisms and engineering methods to systematically identify, assess, and mitigate psychological risks arising from human-AV interactions. To address this gap, this work proposes and validates a systems-theoretic framework for the assessment of psychological safety in autonomous vehicles. First, a comprehensive psychological safety risk model is defined, extending the Systems-Theoretic Accident Model and Processes (STAMP) to incorporate key psychological constructs such as trust, perceived control, predictability, and perceived support. Based on this model, a hazard analysis method (AV-PsySafe) is developed to systematically identify psychological hazards, unsafe control actions, and loss scenarios, while introducing a Psychological Safety Integrity Level (PsySIL) to support risk prioritization. Second, the applicability and relevance of the framework are evaluated through its deployment in realistic autonomous vehicle scenarios. A structured validation approach is implemented, including a methodological guide, standardized analysis templates, and the collection of analyst feedback. The results demonstrate that the framework can be consistently applied by practitioners, producing meaningful insights into psychological risks. Overall, this work establishes both the theoretical foundations and practical feasibility of a unified approach to co-assessing psychological and physical safety in autonomous systems, contributing to more human-centred and trustworthy AV development.

</details>

*Maximilian MacDonald,Chris McCaig,Sean MacAvaney,Matthew Barr,Lauritz Thamsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种综合可持续性评分系统（0-100分），整合了平均碳强度、资源利用率和隐含排放三个维度，并嵌入到现有集群管理界面中，计划通过12周、约35名研究者的受试者内研究评估三种仪表盘条件（原始指标、综合评分、游戏化树形可视化）对行为改变的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算的环境成本持续增长，但用户行为改变仍然有限，现有的可持续性反馈缺乏整合性和激励性，难以促使研究者采取更环保的计算行为。

Method: 设计一个0-100的综合可持续性评分，包含三个维度：碳感知工作负载调度（平均碳强度）、高资源利用率、以及选择被普遍利用不足的硬件（隐含排放）。将该评分系统集成到现有集群管理界面中，并构建三种仪表盘条件（原始指标、综合评分、游戏化树形可视化），计划进行12周受试者内研究。

Result: 完成了评分系统的设计与实现，并规划了12周、约35名研究者的受试者内实验研究方案，但尚未报告实验结果。

Conclusion: 论文还开启了对计算工作"良善性"（goodness）定义的讨论，指出在可持续性评分的语境下，如何界定什么是"好的"计算工作需要进一步深入探讨。

Abstract: The environmental cost of computing continues to grow, yet behaviour change remains limited. We present a composite sustainability score integrating average carbon intensity, resource utilisation, and embodied emissions into a single 0-100 metric designed for gamified feedback. Each component rewards a different dimension of sustainable behaviour: carbon-aware workload shifting, high resource utilisation, and selecting hardware that is commonly underutilised. This scoring system is built into an existing cluster management interface and underpins three dashboard conditions: raw metrics, composite score, and a gamified tree visualisation, which we are planning to evaluate in a 12-week within-subjects study with approximately 35 researchers. Furthermore, we open the discussion on the challenge of defining computational work `goodness' in the context of sustainability scores.

</details>


### [12] [A revised framework for the assessment of psychological safety in autonomous vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18801)
*Yandika Sirgabsou,Benjamin Hardin,François Leblanc,Efi Raili,David Jackson,Pericle Salvini,Lars Kunze,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于STAMP方法论的自动驾驶汽车（AV）心理安全风险评估理论框架，通过用例验证该框架能够系统性地识别导致乘员心理风险的因素，弥补了该领域缺乏正式化风险评估方法的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AV技术发展迅速，但社会接受度仍受心理障碍影响。现有研究虽已证实信任和感知安全是影响AV使用意愿的关键因素，但缺乏正式化的机制来描述人机交互如何导致心理危害，也没有明确指南帮助系统设计者评估和应对AV开发中的心理风险。

Method: 本文在早期提案的基础上扩展了AV心理安全风险评估理论框架，该框架包含两部分：一是涵盖所有心理安全关键概念的扩展风险模型；二是基于系统理论事故模型与过程（STAMP）的评估方法。作者通过一个高度自动化的AV用例场景验证框架的有效性。

Result: 通过高度自动化AV用例场景的演示，框架成功揭示出可能导致乘员心理风险的因素，并提供了如何全面评估心理风险、确定可能导致风险的车辆行为的示例。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为AV心理安全风险评估提供了此前缺失的基础和方法论，有助于推动负责任的AV开发，确保心理安全得到系统性考虑。

Abstract: Despite recent technological progress in the development of autonomous vehicles (AVs), their societal acceptability remains a subject of debate as recent research findings point to psychological roadblocks. Concerns arise not only for physical safety but also for potential psychological risks resulting from human interaction with AVs. Psychological concepts such as trust, and perceived safety are well-studied in this context and are found to be determinant factors for the intention to use AVs. Unfortunately, there has been no formalization of the mechanism by which human interaction with AVs may lead to psychological hazards, threatening trust, perceived safety, and acceptability. Furthermore, there has been little prior research that conceptualizes the severity of psychological risk in AVs, and there are no clear guidelines for a systems designer on how to assess and address psychological risk in the AV development context. To address these limitations, this paper extends a theoretical framework for AV psychological safety risk assessment based on an early proposal. The proposed framework consists of an extended risk model for psychological safety including all the key concepts related to psychological safety in AVs, and an assessment method based on the Systems-Theoretic Accident Model and Processes (STAMP). We demonstrate the usefulness of the theoretical framework through a highly automated AV use case scenario, uncovering factors which may lead to psychological risk for an occupant. The use cases provide examples of how to use the framework to extensively evaluate psychological risk and determine vehicle behaviour that could lead to this risk. By developing a theoretical framework for AV psychological safety risk assessment, we provide a foundation and a method that were previously lacking to better enable responsible AV development regarding psychological safety.

</details>


### [13] [TractorBeam: Personalized AI Sensemaking Support via Collaborative Machine Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18994)
*Sireesh Gururaja,Jordan Taylor,Emma Strubell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: TractorBeam是一个基于浏览器扩展的混合主动系统，通过协作注解作为界面隐喻来辅助文档理解，将语言模型输出重新定义为PDF文档上的建议高亮，解决了事实性和溯源问题，并支持探索性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于语言模型的文档问答系统虽然流行，但仍存在事实性错误和溯源不清的问题，且倾向于鼓励确认性而非探索性研究。

Method: 提出协作机器注解（collaborative machine annotation）的概念，将LM输出以建议高亮的形式呈现在PDF文档的上下文中，使用户能在文档语境中迭代构建心智图式和查询，实现混合主动的文档理解。

Result: 初步用户研究表明，所有参与者认为TractorBeam能够帮助他们评估并迭代改进模型对其预期高亮的反映，部分参与者发现了促使其重新思考原始图式的建议。

Conclusion: TractorBeam表明，促进用户在单个文档上进行探索性研究的系统可以实现可验证的理解，并可作为跨大规模语料库工具的补充。

Abstract: Language model-based systems which allow asking questions of documents have become popular tools for sensemaking. Despite their implied capability, these systems still suffer from issues of factuality and provenance, while encouraging confirmatory, rather than exploratory, research. We present TractorBeam, a browser extension-based mixed-initiative system that uses collaborative annotation as an interface metaphor for sensemaking, re-framing language model (LM) outputs as suggested highlights in a process that we call \textit{collaborative machine annotation}. This metaphor allows us to present LM results in-context on PDF documents, directly addressing concerns of provenance and factuality, while allowing users to iteratively construct mental schemas and queries for language models directly in the context of a document. In a preliminary user study, all of our participants felt that TractorBeam enabled them evaluate and iteratively improve the model's reflection of their intended highlighting, and several found suggestions that made them reconsider their original schema. TractorBeam suggests that systems that facilitate exploratory research on individual documents may lead to verifiable sensemaking for users and complement tools that work across broader corpora.

</details>


### [14] [When Readability and Source Retention Diverge: An Evaluability Gap in AI Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19083)
*Chenchen Mao,Hanjing Shi,Haiyan Jia,Emily Wegrzyn,Dominic DiFranzo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了可读性AI输出的"可评估性差距"——即使展示了源文本，整体质量判断也可能无法反映输出保留的内容。通过2×2实验（N=306）发现，渲染方式与源文本条件的交互作用显著影响感知质量：在简单叙事中，保真导向输出被评为更高；而在复杂哲学散文中，无明显差异。任务表现信任是披露意愿的最接近相关因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可读的AI输出可能产生"可评估性差距"：即使用户可以看到源文本，其整体质量判断也可能无法反映输出实际保留了多少源内容。这一差距可能影响用户对翻译系统的信任和使用意愿，因此需要系统研究其机制。

Method: 采用2（渲染方式：LLM可读性导向vs研究者保真导向）×2（源文本条件：简单生成叙事vs复杂文学哲学散文）的焦点实验设计（N=306），使用TransLingo平台，结合描述性刺激审计和因子分析，并运用理论排序的SEM结构方程模型评估感知质量、感知智能、拟人化归因、任务表现信任与披露意愿之间的关系。

Result: 保真导向输出在两种源文本条件下都保留了更多源内容。因子分析显示渲染方式与源文本条件存在显著交互作用：简单叙事中保真导向输出被评为更高，复杂散文中无显著渲染差异。类似模式也出现在感知智能、代理导向拟人化归因和任务表现信任中。SEM表明任务表现信任是披露意愿的最接近相关因素。

Conclusion: 评级模式区分了"源文本可获取性"与"源文本可评估性"：对于复杂刺激，展示源文本并不能确保整体质量评分反映保留内容的差异。此外，对翻译输出的评估支持与对数据处理的信任支持是分离的，即用户对输出质量的判断与决定将何种个人文本托付给系统的决策是不同的维度。

Abstract: Readable AI output can leave an evaluability gap: even when the source is shown, an overall-quality judgment may not reflect what an output preserves. We investigated how source-text condition and output rendering relate to perceived translation quality, and how output and system appraisals relate to trust and stated disclosure willingness in a plain-text interface. A focal 2 * 2 comparison (N=306) using TransLingo examined simple generated narratives and complex literary-philosophical prose alongside LLM-generated readability-oriented outputs and researcher-revised fidelity-oriented outputs. A descriptive stimulus audit indicated greater source retention in fidelity-oriented outputs in both source-text conditions. Factorial analyses showed a significant rendering-by-source-text-condition interaction in perceived quality. Participants rated fidelity-oriented outputs higher than readability-oriented outputs for the simple narratives, whereas no reliable rendering difference emerged for the complex prose. A corresponding source-condition-dependent pattern was observed for perceived intelligence, agency-oriented anthropomorphic attribution, and task-performance trust. A separate theory-ordered appraisal-structure SEM characterized concurrent associations among perceived quality, perceived intelligence, agency-oriented anthropomorphic attribution, task-performance trust, and stated disclosure willingness across six domains, with task-performance trust as the proximal correlate of stated willingness. The observed rating pattern distinguishes source access from source evaluability: for the complex stimuli, displaying the source did not ensure that one overall-quality rating reflected differences in retained content. It also separates support for evaluating translation output from data-handling support for decisions about what personal text to entrust to a system.

</details>


### [15] [Trade-offs in Data Color Palette Design Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19148)
*Shiyi He,Andrew M McNutt*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过40名设计师的出声思维众包实验，探讨了四种不同交互模式的调色板工具如何影响设计过程与结果。研究发现工具差异在无障碍约束任务中更为明显，且某些工具即使在不强制要求无障碍的情况下也能产生更具无障碍友好性的调色板并激发设计师的无障碍思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调色板工具在数据设计中被广泛使用，但不同工具的交互机制如何影响设计结果和设计过程（特别是在无障碍和美学的平衡上）目前知之甚少，缺乏跨语境下的系统性研究。

Method: 采用探索性出声思维（think-aloud）众包研究方法，招募40名自称设计师的参与者，将其分配到四种覆盖不同交互模式的调色板工具中，完成一系列围绕无障碍和美学导向的设计任务，并对过程与结果进行观察分析。

Result: 发现两个初步模式：其一，工具之间的差异在无障碍约束任务中表现更为显著；其二，即使任务未明确要求无障碍，某些工具仍能生成更具无障碍友好性的调色板，并促使设计师更多地进行无障碍相关思考。

Conclusion: 在调色板工具这一类型中，系统设计通过内置功能直接影响输出结果，同时通过引导设计师的注意力朝向特定约束和设计考虑，间接塑造设计过程与决策。

Abstract: Designing a color palette for data requires designers to balance multiple constraints, including accessibility and aesthetics. Color palette tools support this process through features including direct manipulation, automated palette generation and evaluation, previews, and so on. Despite their prominence, relatively little is known about how these different mechanisms shape design across contexts. We conducted an exploratory think-aloud crowd work study with 40 self-identified designers. Each participant used one of four palette tools selected to span different interaction modalities to complete a series of accessibility- and aesthetics-oriented design tasks. We observed two preliminary patterns. First, tool differences were more pronounced in accessibility-constrained tasks. Second, even when accessibility was not explicitly required, some tools produced more accessibility-friendly palettes and prompted more accessibility-oriented thinking. In this tool genre, then, system design shapes outcomes both via built-in functionality, as well as by directing designers' attention toward particular constraints and design considerations.

</details>

### [16] [Entropy-Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18147)
*Ran Ben Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Michael Mitzenmacher,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵约束自适应随机量化（ECASQ）问题，在熵预算和无偏约束下联合选择自适应量化值以最小化均方误差（MSE），并给出了最优动态规划算法（O(sd²)时间，O(d²)空间）以及GPU友好的近似算法（O(sd²)时间，O(d)空间）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无偏量化方法（包括ASQ）在选择量化值时没有考虑后续的无损熵编码阶段，导致压缩精度仍有提升空间。

Method: 将问题形式化为联合优化问题（ECASQ），提出两种算法：一是最优动态规划（O(sd²)时间和O(d²)空间），二是GPU友好的近似动态规划（O(sd²)时间和O(d)空间），后者保证解的质量不超过使用少一位熵的最优解，并附带有迭代精化过程。

Result: 近似算法在实验中能够取得接近最优的结果，同时相比最优求解器保持了显著的速度优势。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在量化阶段联合考虑熵编码约束能够进一步提升量化精度，为实用的量化系统提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive stochastic quantization (ASQ) is a recently introduced quantization approach that optimizes the Mean Squared Error (MSE) for a given input while preserving unbiasedness. It is designed to alleviate the communication and memory bottlenecks of modern data and machine learning workloads, including model, gradient, and KV-cache compression and nearest-neighbor search. Further, practical systems can then compress quantized data with a lossless entropy encoder. However, existing unbiased methods, including ASQ, choose their quantization values without considering this later encoding stage, leaving accuracy on the table.
  We formulate the Entropy Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization (ECASQ) problem, which jointly selects adaptive quantization values to minimize MSE under an entropy budget and an unbiasedness constraint. We give an optimal dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d^2)$ space for a length-d vector and at most s quantization values, and a GPU-friendly approximate dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d)$ space. The approximation guarantees that the solution has an MSE no larger than the optimal solution that uses one fewer bit of entropy per entry. We also provide an iterative refinement procedure for the approximation solution that, in our experiments, yields near-optimal results while retaining a substantial speed advantage over our solver for the optimal solution.

</details>


### [17] [Towards Reversible Forgetting: Managing Obsolete Knowledge in Continual Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18177)
*Nilutpaul Sarker Yash,Tirtho Roy,Ushashi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可逆遗忘"概念框架，用于企业AI代理在非平稳环境中的持续学习。不同于传统持续学习将遗忘视为失败，该框架通过活跃、休眠、退役三种记忆状态及再激活转换，实现选择性遗忘。文中以滞回可逆记忆控制器（HRMC）实例化该框架，使用非对称阈值防止状态振荡，并通过影子模式测试和策略门控管理记忆恢复，以减少过时知识对决策的负面影响。

该内容未通过合规审查，已隐藏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习将遗忘视为需要避免的失败，但企业AI代理运行于非平稳环境（客户、政策、工具、工作流、法规、市场条件不断变化），盲目保留旧知识会导致过时信息影响决策，产生负迁移和运营风险。因此需要一个更完整的记忆管理框架。

Method: 提出可逆遗忘概念框架，包含三种操作记忆状态（活跃、休眠、退役）及再激活转换机制。实例化为滞回可逆记忆控制器（HRMC）：积累相关性证据、用非对称阈值避免状态振荡、在影子模式下测试再激活、通过策略门控退役。

Result: 该框架能够减少过时信息对决策的影响，同时不将临时抑制与永久删除混为一谈。以金融领域为例验证思路：在一种市场制度下有用的知识在另一种制度下可能有害，但类似条件重现时可重新恢复相关性。

Conclusion: 可逆遗忘为企业AI代理提供了比传统"永久保留"或"永久删除"更精细的记忆管理方式，在动态环境中实现了知识保留与遗忘的平衡。

Abstract: Continual learning has traditionally treated forgetting as a failure, emphasizing preservation of previously acquired knowledge as environments evolve. We argue that this objective is incomplete for enterprise AI agents operating in non-stationary environments, where customers, policies, tools, workflows, regulations, and market conditions change over time. Indiscriminate retention can allow obsolete knowledge to influence decisions, creating negative transfer and operational risk. We therefore propose reversible forgetting: a conceptual framework with three operational memory states: active, dormant, and retired, and a reactivation transition that can restore dormant knowledge when its relevance returns. We instantiate the framework as a Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller that accumulates relevance evidence, uses asymmetric thresholds to prevent state oscillation, tests reactivation in shadow mode, and gates retirement through policy. The framework reduces the influence of obsolete information without conflating temporary suppression with permanent erasure. Finance illustrates the idea: knowledge useful under one market regime may become harmful under another yet regain relevance when similar conditions recur.

</details>


### [18] [Accelerating Visual On-Policy Distillation with Batched Speculative Jacobi Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18183)
*Bingqi Shan,Zhehao Yu,Kenhong Lin,Baoquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HB-SJD是一种用于视觉on-policy蒸馏的批处理推测式Jacobi解码回滚后端，通过允许每个图像独立推进解码进度并动态切换Full/Compact执行模式，在显著降低回滚和训练时间的同时保持蒸馏学生的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉on-policy蒸馏（OPD）虽然能提升紧凑视觉自回归模型的训练效果，但学生模型仍需逐token自回归解码生成在线轨迹，给每个训练步骤带来高昂的额外计算成本。现有的推测式Jacobi解码（SJD）虽能并行处理多个token且无需辅助草稿模型，但仅适用于单序列推理场景，无法直接用于批量训练。

Method: HB-SJD作为批量SJD回滚后端：1) 允许每张图像根据自身解码进度独立推进；2) 不同序列位置的图像仍在批量模型前向中统一验证；3) 当图像完成解码后，在Full和Compact执行模式间动态切换，以降低后续回滚轮次的成本；4) 仅替换学生回滚后端，不改变教师模型、蒸馏目标和优化流程。

Result: 在LlamaGen上的实验表明，HB-SJD大幅减少了回滚时间和端到端训练时间，同时保持了蒸馏学生的生成质量。

Conclusion: HB-SJD成功将推测式Jacobi解码扩展到批量视觉OPD训练场景，在有效降低训练开销的同时不牺牲生成质量，为大规模视觉蒸馏训练提供了更高效的在线回滚方案。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation (OPD) improves the training of compact visual autoregressive models by learning from trajectories generated by the current student. However, these online rollouts are still produced token by token with autoregressive decoding, which adds substantial cost to every on-policy training step. Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) provides an alternative because it can process multiple tokens in parallel without an auxiliary draft model, but the original method is designed for single-sequence inference. We introduce HB-SJD, a batched SJD rollout backend for visual OPD. HB-SJD allows each image to advance independently according to its own decoding progress, while images at different sequence positions are still verified in batched model forwards. As images finish, HB-SJD switches between Full and Compact execution to reduce the cost of later rollout rounds. HB-SJD only replaces the student rollout backend and leaves the teacher, distillation objective, and optimization procedure unchanged. Experiments with LlamaGen show that HB-SJD substantially reduces rollout and end-to-end training time while preserving the generation quality of the distilled student.

</details>


### [19] [H$^2$EDL: Hyper Evidential Deep Learning for Hierarchical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18185)
*Yuanye Liu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H²EDL模型，利用标签树的分层结构作为hyperdomain，通过每个分支节点上的局部Dirichlet意见构建线性大小的焦点族，统一了平坦证据分类器与分层分类器的优势。该模型既可作为保持层级一致性的分层分类器，也可视为合法的树结构超意见分布，在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345上比交叉熵基线减少约一半的校准误差，且越深层级和越大训练预算下改进越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别中，模型可能对粗粒度概念有把握但对细粒度子类不确定。现有方法各有缺陷：平坦证据分类器只用一个vacuity量化整体无知，无法表达中间概念；分层分类器传播点概率但没有证据概念。而通用超意见形式在标签数量上呈指数级，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: 观察到标签分类树本身就是缺失的hyperdomain：其子树和叶单例构成线性大小的焦点族，每个分支节点上的局部Dirichlet意见可以闭式推导出所有复合类别的质量。该模型H²EDL使用相同参数提供两种互补解释：预测上作为保持层级一致性的分层分类器，概率上作为合法的树结构超意见分布，节点的质量表示到达该节点但不足以进一步细分的置信度。

Result: 在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345数据集上，H²EDL相比交叉熵基线将校准误差减少约一半，改进在更深层级和更大训练预算下更加显著。

Conclusion: 通过利用标签树结构作为天然hyperdomain，H²EDL有效弥合了不确定性量化与层级分类之间的鸿沟，实现了在细粒度层级上的结构化解释和可扩展的不确定性建模。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition often involves hierarchical label spaces, where a model may be confident about a coarse semantic concept while remaining uncertain among its descendant classes. Such structured ambiguity requires uncertainty representations that capture both fine-grained classes and intermediate concepts. However, existing tools each capture only half of it: flat evidential classifiers quantify total ignorance with a single vacuity on the leaf frame, and hierarchical classifiers propagate point probabilities with no notion of evidence. Hyper-opinions would unify the two, but their general form is exponential in the label count, and existing hyper-evidential networks either require composite labels to be supplied in the training data or read them off an unstructured weight pattern, with no principled notion of which composites deserve mass. We observe that the taxonomy itself is the missing hyperdomain. Its subtrees and leaf singletons form a linear-size focal family, and one local Dirichlet opinion per branching node induces every composite mass in closed form. The resulting model, H$^2$EDL, can be interpreted in two complementary ways using the same set of parameters. From a prediction perspective, it functions as a hierarchical classifier that preserves consistency across different levels of the label tree. From a probabilistic perspective, it defines a valid tree-structured hyper-opinion, where the mass assigned to each node represents the belief that reaches that node but does not provide sufficient confidence to further specialize into its descendants. On FGVC-Aircraft and DERM12345, H$^2$EDL reduces calibration error by approximately half compared with cross-entropy baselines, with the improvement becoming more pronounced at deeper hierarchy levels and under larger training budgets.

</details>


### [20] [What Can Artificial Intelligence Learn from Medicine? Generative Analogies and Reliable Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18186)
*Emanuele Ratti,Lena Zuchowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Hesse的理论工具，将医学临床转化与机器学习（ML）系统构建之间的类比刻画为一种"生成性类比"，并在此基础上厘清临床转化在认识论和方法论上的保证条件，论证这些条件可以类比地适用于ML领域，进而提出一种新的ML可靠主义观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习在医学中得到了广泛应用，但其认识论和方法论基础仍不明确。学界虽已提出以临床转化标准为模板来规范ML，但这一类比的具体性质及其所涉及的认识论保证条件尚未被精确阐明，需要系统性的理论分析。

Method: 作者借助Hesse关于类比（analogy）的理论工具，将医学临床转化过程与ML系统构建过程之间的平行关系刻画为一种"生成性类比"（generative analogy），进而通过可靠主义（reliabilism）视角对临床转化的认识论和方法论保证条件进行具体阐释，并论证这些条件如何类比地适用于ML语境。

Result: 论文精确识别了临床转化过程中通常仅在类比中被提及的认识论和方法论保证条件，并以可靠主义术语对其加以解释；同时展示这些保证如何在类比意义上适用于ML，从而提出一种与现有哲学中AI可靠主义观点不同但相容的新型ML可靠主义。

Conclusion: 通过系统性的类比分析，本文为ML的认识论和方法论基础提供了更精确的理论支撑，提出了一种新的ML可靠主义框架，丰富了人工智能哲学中关于ML可靠性问题的讨论。

Abstract: In the past few years, machine learning (ML) has been widely (and to an extent, successfully) implemented in medicine. However, uncertainties surrounding ML have made it difficult to establish the bases of its epistemic and methodological warrants. In the literature, a parallel has been drawn between medicine and ML, suggesting that we should model epistemic and methodological standards for ML on the standards of clinical translation. By developing tools from Hesse work, we characterise the nature of this parallel as a generative analogy between the process of clinical translation and the process of building ML systems. We identify more precisely the epistemic and methodological warrants of clinical translation that are typically only mentioned when appealing to the analogy, and we show in which sense such warrants apply analogically to the context of ML. In particular, we interpret warrants of clinical translation in reliabilist terms, and we show how this can inform a new form of ML reliabilism, which is distinct from (though compatible with) existing reliabilist accounts in philosophy of AI.

</details>


### [21] [A systematic review of machine learning techniques to address diagnosis and treatment of autism: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18188)
*Rafael Muñoz-Terol,Jesús Peral,Sandra Amador,David Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2017至2023年间55项关于机器学习在自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）研究中应用的研究，发现监督学习方法占主导地位，深度学习的应用随着数据可用性增加而不断扩展，并指出了多模态数据整合和跨学科合作是未来发展的关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD的成因尚不明确，早期诊断至关重要。通过系统梳理近年机器学习在ASD研究中的应用，识别趋势、技术及数据集，以推动诊断和治疗效果的提升。

Method: 对2017至2023年间发表的55项研究进行系统性回顾，分析机器学习在ASD研究中应用的趋势、技术和数据集，重点关注监督学习、深度学习以及混合方法的演变。

Result: 监督学习方法在ASD诊断中占据主导地位，深度学习的作用随数据可用性增加而扩大。混合方法（结合无监督学习、深度学习和模糊逻辑）被视为未来值得关注的趋势。此外，整合遗传和临床等复杂数据，以及引入可穿戴设备和生物传感器等创新数据源，有望实现更全面的ASD监测和理解。

Conclusion: 解决当前ASD研究的挑战需要跨学科合作和针对ASD的扩展数据集。未来的机器学习模型将从更广泛的多模态数据整合中受益，以更全面地应对ASD的复杂性。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is a developmental disability characterized by challenges in social interaction and communication. As the causes of ASD remain unclear, identifying relevant features and hidden correlations is crucial for early diagnosis. This systematic review evaluates 55 studies from 2017 to 2023 on the application of machine learning (ML) techniques to ASD. The primary objective is to examine recent ML applications in ASD research, identifying trends, techniques, and datasets that enhance diagnosis and treatment. Supervised learning methods dominate, as they align well with ASD diagnostic needs; however, the role of deep learning is expanding with greater data availability. Emerging techniques based on hybrid methods, where unsupervised, deep learning, and fuzzy logic could be included, will be interesting to observe in the future. The review highlights key challenges and opportunities, particularly the need for models that can integrate complex data -such as genetic and clinical information- to improve diagnostic accuracy and treatment outcomes. Additionally, incorporating innovative data sources, like wearable devices and biometric sensors, could enable continuous and non-intrusive monitoring, providing a more holistic understanding of ASD. Findings emphasize that addressing current challenges requires interdisciplinary collaboration and expanded datasets tailored to ASD. Future ML models will benefit from broader multimodal data integration, enabling researchers to more comprehensively address the complexities of ASD.

</details>


### [22] [Safe Domain Adaptation for Physics: Overcoming Nuisances, Label Shifts, and Simulation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18190)
*Ivan Kharuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了物理应用中域自适应方法的局限性，提出了自适应域自适应（adaptive domain adaptation）方法以处理模拟与实验数据之间的物理偏移和能谱分布差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统域自适应假设两个域仅存在噪声差异且目标量分布相同，但在物理领域这两个假设均不成立：模拟可能在物理上出错，目标量（如能谱、红移分布）的分布本身往往就是测量对象。这种失配会导致偏差替代问题。

Method: 在一个玩具空气簇射基准上，分别或组合地引入探测器响应噪声、物理模拟偏移和能谱偏移，研究对抗式域自适应的影响。提出自适应域自适应方法，通过对模拟事件重新加权，使域自适应专注于真实的物理失配。同时提出无标签模型选择规则以确定接近最优的工作点。

Result: 标准对抗式域自适应能处理条件偏移，但当两个能谱不一致时会对齐它们，将无控制的偏差替换为以模拟先验为锚的偏差。所提出的自适应域自适应方法避免了对能谱的对齐，并通过无标签模型选择规则实现接近最优的性能。

Conclusion: 在物理应用中，标准域自适应方法在目标分布本身是测量量时会产生系统性偏差，需要针对物理失配重加权的自适应策略和相应的模型选择机制。

Abstract: Domain adaptation is widely used to make neural networks trained on simulations applicable to experimental data. Its premise is that the two domains differ only in nuisances, and that the quantity of interest is distributed identically in both. In physics neither assumption holds: simulations can be wrong about the physics, and the distribution of the target quantity - an energy spectrum, a redshift distribution - is often the measurement itself. We study the consequences of such mismatches on a toy air-shower benchmark in which a detector-response nuisance, a physical simulation shift, and an energy-spectrum shift can be switched on separately or together. Standard adversarial adaptation handles the conditional shifts, but once the two spectra differ it aligns them, replacing an uncontrolled bias by one anchored on the simulation prior. We present adaptive domain adaptation, which reweights the simulated events so as to focus domain adaptation on the genuine physical mismatch alone. Since the predicted spectrum depends on model training configuration, we provide a label-free model selection rule for selecting the near-the-best operation point.

</details>


### [23] [ChiroEcho: extending automated bat vocalisation classification beyond the learned taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18191)
*Burooj Ghani,Welmoed Eversteijn,Milan van Hirtum,Juan Sebastián Cañas,Vincent J. Kalkman,Dan Stowell,A. Leonie Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个深度学习框架，通过联合预测蝙蝠的物种和属，并在推理阶段将属预测与地理物种分布相结合，从而在不扩展分类器训练标签的情况下解决未见过的物种分类问题。该框架将欧洲蝙蝠分类的操作覆盖率从73%（35种）提升至85%（41种，覆盖48种原生欧洲蝙蝠中的41种）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠是欧洲生态健康的关键指示物种，但声学回波定位叫声因行为和环境而变化且种间重叠，自动识别困难。现有分类器受限于训练数据中的物种标签（taxonomy），无法识别未学习过的物种，因此需要一种方法在不扩展训练标签的前提下利用地理分布信息扩展有效分类范围。

Method: 开发了一个深度学习框架，联合预测物种和属两个层级。在推理阶段，将属预测结果与地理物种分布数据结合：若某属在特定区域仅有一种物种，则可将该属预测解析为具体物种，即使该物种不在训练分类器中。同时进行了闭集分类评估、稀有物种性能估计不稳定性分析，以及受控的留出（held-out）验证实验。

Result: 使用涵盖35种欧洲蝙蝠物种的录音数据进行评估。结果表明：稀有物种因有限的评估数据导致性能估计不稳定；留出实验证明属预测结合地理位置可以恢复物种级标签；地理信息解析使操作覆盖率从35种扩展至41种，覆盖率从73%提升至85%，是目前报道的欧洲自动化蝙蝠分类中最广的操作覆盖范围。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了一种通用思路：通过粗粒度预测（属）结合透明的外部约束（地理分布），可以解析出细粒度（物种）未见类别的标签。这不仅为蝙蝠监测提供了更广覆盖的自动化工具，也为其他领域中"未见细粒度类别解析"问题提供了概念验证。

Abstract: Bats are key indicators of ecosystem health and are protected throughout Europe, making reliable population monitoring a conservation priority. Their cryptic nocturnal lifestyle makes passive acoustic monitoring essential, yet automated identification remains difficult as echolocation calls vary with behaviour and environment and overlap among species. We present a deep learning framework that jointly predicts species and genus and combines genus predictions with geographic species distributions at inference. When only one species of a predicted genus occurs in a region, the framework can resolve species absent from the learned taxonomy. This reframes geographic information as a means of extending, rather than constraining, a classifier's effective taxonomy. Using recordings spanning 35 European bat species, we evaluate closed-set classification, examine the instability of performance estimates for sparsely represented species, and conduct a controlled held-out proof-of-principle experiment. The rare-species analysis shows how limited evaluation data can obscure species-level performance, while the held-out experiment shows that genus predictions and location can recover labels unavailable to the species head. Geographic resolution extends operational coverage from 35 to 41 of the 48 native European bat species, increasing coverage from 73% to 85%. To our knowledge, this is the broadest operational coverage reported for automated European bat classification. More broadly, the bat framework provides proof of principle for resolving unseen fine-grained classes by combining coarse predictions with transparent external constraints.

</details>


### [24] [Think Shallow, Solve Deep: Controlling Recurrent Dynamics for Reliable Test-Time Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18222)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了递归深度推理器在测试时增加迭代次数的行为，发现训练算子的有限时间动力学状态（稳定、临界或漂移）可以预测深度增加的效果。给出了深度安全性的充分条件，并通过实验验证了稳定算子不会因增加深度而退化，且在某些任务上还能提升困难未见实例的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归深度推理器旨在通过测试时增加迭代次数来解决更难的问题，但额外迭代可能改善、保持不变或恶化答案。这种不确定性阻碍了测试时深度扩展的可靠应用，因此需要理解何种条件下增加深度是有益的。

Method: 1) 定义并估计训练算子的有限时间动力学状态（稳定/临界/漂移）；2) 给出深度安全性的充分条件——当每步位移相对解码器余量较小时，进一步迭代不会改变解码答案；3) 通过终端不动点目标函数控制动力学状态；4) 提出四个测试时深度的操作性标准并用其归类失败模式。

Result: 在800个未增强样本的算法任务上，稳定算子不会随深度增加而退化，且在部分任务上将更难未见实例的准确率从0.19提升至0.34（超越训练范围）。去除终端不动点目标会导致漂移并消除增益，添加该目标能使进位传播任务实现深度安全的外推。Huginn-3.5B属于非稳定家族。

Conclusion: 训练算子的有限时间动力学状态是预测测试时深度行为的关键指标。终端不动点目标可以有效控制系统动力学，实现深度安全的推理扩展，但大型模型如Huginn-3.5B仍属于非稳定家族，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [25] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MixerLoop方法，仅循环重复Gated DeltaNet混合器而稀疏FFN只执行一次，在15M和110M参数规模下相比全块循环更具优势，并在减少45.9%循环骨干投影FLOPs的同时保留大部分性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型通常重复整个层堆栈（混合器+FFN），但混合器和FFN执行不同的操作且有不同成本。作者提出更精准的问题：循环中究竟应该循环什么？通过迭代传输秩（ITR）分析循环的价值所在。

Method: 提出迭代传输秩（ITR）框架描述累积影响轨迹，边际ITR描述非冗余影响。基于此提出MixerLoop架构，重复每个Gated DeltaNet混合器但FFN仅执行一次。使用有限上下文偏移干预测试验证循环应用是否产生显著且有益的变化。在相同数据、初始化和架构下对比15M和110M参数规模。

Result: MixerLoop在15M参数下超越FullLoop（全块循环），在110M参数下保留了FullLoop CORE改进的41.5%，同时将循环骨干投影FLOPs减少45.9%。

Conclusion: 循环深度的益处可以在不重复执行密集FFN的情况下保留，MixerLoop通过只循环混合器部分实现了计算效率与性能的更好平衡。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>


### [26] [Classifying Directional Trajectories Near Criticality in the Three-State Majority-Vote Model with Deep Belief Networks and Bidirectional GRUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18235)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种层次化架构，结合深度信念网络（DBN）和双向门控循环单元（Bi-GRU），用于区分三态多数投票模型（MV3）中的四种动态轨迹类型（从无序接近、从有序接近、离开到无序、离开到有序）。DBN将晶格快照编码为81维潜在向量，Bi-GRU基于时序编码实现近乎完美的四分类，并能在实时滑动窗口中感知系统的动态状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态配置训练的模型无法完全解析方向性时间结构，需要一种能够区分动态轨迹类型（而非仅静态状态）的架构，以检测和分类基于智能体的观点动态模型中的临界转变。

Method: 首先使用Gaussian-Bernoulli RBM输入层以无监督方式对DBN进行预训练（架构784→4096→225→81），将MV3模型的晶格快照编码为81维潜在向量；然后训练一个两层Bi-GRU分类器，输入为DBN编码的快照序列（长度T=50），在隐藏状态空间对四种轨迹类型进行分类；最后通过滑动窗口方式将训练好的Bi-GRU应用于连续的MV3动态过程。

Result: t-SNE分析显示DBN潜在空间仅对四种轨迹类型实现部分分离；而Bi-GRU分类器在隐藏状态空间中实现了四种轨迹类型的近乎完美分离（训练集和测试集均验证）；滑动窗口应用证实Bi-GRU能够实时感知系统当前的动态状态。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种分层架构，能够有效检测和分类基于智能体的观点动力学模型中的临界转变，为复杂系统的动态状态感知提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [27] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClosureBench，一个基于程序验证的构造式组合图关系推理基准，可动态生成实例以避免数据污染；通过评估多种模型发现三个关键结论：它能直接测量记忆化问题、模型在组合推理中存在图理解瓶颈、程序合成微调可显著提升组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图关系推理基准使用固定测试集，容易被数据污染影响评估公平性；同时模型在组合推理上的真实能力难以准确衡量。需要一个能动态生成、机器可验证的基准来可靠评估模型的组合图推理能力。

Method: 构建ClosureBench基准，基于Ein张量逻辑语言执行程序来生成实例的参考答案，确保机器验证正确性；包含26个任务类别、3个组合层级（L1-L3），沿图规模、边密度和查询深度三个独立轴控制难度；评估从1.5B到前沿模型的广泛模型，并设计程序合成微调实验。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）在固定测试集上微调的模型在seen vs fresh实例上存在19.3个百分点的差距，静态基准无法揭示这一记忆化问题；（2）准确率随图规模和查询深度增加而下降且二者交互，模型先在自然语言图描述上出错再在错误图上推理，该瓶颈在JSON边列表和邻接矩阵输入下依然存在；（3）4B模型通过微调输出可执行程序几乎不随组合层级下降，达到94.3%的held-out准确率，且分别验证了Ein和Python+NetworkX两种程序目标。

Conclusion: ClosureBench提供了可动态生成、抗数据污染的构造式基准；程序合成而非直接答案预测是提升组合图推理能力的有效途径，且最优模型的核心瓶颈在于从自然语言正确解析图结构，而非后续推理本身。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [28] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现生物与人工神经网络之间的表征对齐是不对称的，并提出通过谱正则化调整表征几何结构来系统地影响双向对齐。在自监督对比视觉模型上的实验表明，该方法在正向可预测性仅小幅下降的情况下，将反向可预测性大幅提升，双向可预测性相对提升55%，且中间谱指数下正反向可预测性近似对称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明生物与人工神经网络之间的表征对齐存在不对称性：模型表征预测神经响应远好于神经响应预测模型表征。这一不对称性引出了一个关键问题：表征几何结构是否会影响双向表征对齐？作者旨在探索通过调控表征几何来系统性地改善双向对齐。

Method: 作者开发了一个将谱正则化与双向可预测性分析相结合的计算框架，通过在训练过程中引导表征的谱几何结构来检验其对双向对齐的影响，并使用自监督对比视觉模型作为初步验证平台。

Result: 引导表征谱几何显著提高了反向可预测性，同时对正向可预测性仅有适度降低，双向可预测性相对提升55%。改进伴随着有效维度降低和共享表征子空间的重组，在中间谱指数下正反向可预测性变得近似对称。

Conclusion: 研究证明了表征几何可以被系统地操控以调节生物与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐，为理解两种网络之间信息共享机制提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [29] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGMA框架，结合大语言模型和多目标强化学习实现自适应交通信号控制，通过自然语言指令生成优先级向量，并利用旋转增强和离线到在线学习提升泛化性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL交通信号控制方法存在三大问题：目标函数固定无法适应动态需求变化、缺乏对紧急车辆优先级的灵活处理、跨交叉口几何泛化能力不足。

Method: SIGMA包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用LLM将自然语言紧急指令转换为多目标actor-critic控制器的优先级向量，免去人工奖励设计；(2) 采用旋转增强技术使模型学习方向不变性，提升对四向交叉口的迁移能力；(3) 使用离线到在线学习策略保证稳定初始化并逐步适应流量变化。

Result: 在SUMO中对印度Kolkata四个城区交叉口进行评估，与固定时长、感应控制和DQN控制器相比，SIGMA在平均/紧急等待时间、队列长度和吞吐量等指标上均表现更优。消融实验验证了组件鲁棒性，bootstrap统计验证了可靠性属性。

Conclusion: SIGMA提供了可靠的语言引导式多目标交通控制系统，具备紧急服务水平、优雅降级和需求敏感性等可靠性保证，且通过了统计显著性的验证。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [30] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，性能提升究竟源于学生学到了参考信息，还是仅仅恢复了基础模型已有的推理行为。通过对Qwen3系列模型（1.7B-8B）在科学和数学数据集上的实验，发现正确的参考并未提供一致的性能增益，学生即使使用错误的参考也能提升性能，且模型预测更倾向于与基础模型的思维行为对齐而非参考信息。因此，仅靠性能提升和分布对齐无法判断特权参考信息在OPSD中是否真正被学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD是一种在同策略上利用特权参考信息进行token级监督的自蒸馏方法。关键疑问在于性能提升的来源：是学生真正学到了参考中的信息，还是仅仅恢复了模型自身已有的推理能力。这一问题的答案决定了OPSD方法的解释性和可靠性。

Method: 提出一个分析框架，将参考信息引起的监督与不带参考的教师监督分开，分别度量两者与学生预测变化的对齐程度。使用Qwen3模型（1.7B-8B）在科学和数学数据集上进行OPSD实验，并通过其他问题的解作为对照来验证对齐结果。

Result: 正确的参考在不同教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集上并未提供一致性的性能增益。学生可以在没有正确参考的情况下提升性能，且其他问题的解在多个数学推理基准上甚至优于正确解。学生的预测与基础模型思维行为的对齐强于与参考监督的对齐，但对照组的其他问题也能复现大部分对齐效果。更强的正确参考对齐并不稳定地对应更大的性能增益。

Conclusion: 性能提升和分布对齐这两类指标均无法单独确定特权参考信息在OPSD中对学生学习的实际贡献，需要新的分析途径来揭示OPSD的学习机制。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

</details>


### [31] [SingularClip: Preventing Spectral Collapse to Maintain Plasticity in Continual and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18319)
*Tyler Kastner,Nimrod De La Vega,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经网络在非平稳任务训练中因权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长而丧失可塑性，提出SingularClip方法通过周期性裁剪所有权重矩阵的奇异值来缓解该问题，并在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的多种任务中验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳任务训练中常常丧失拟合新目标的能力（即可塑性损失），作者希望找到可塑性损失的新来源并加以缓解。

Method: 作者通过经验与理论分析，识别出权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长是导致可塑性丧失的新因素，并提出SingularClip方法，周期性对所有权重矩阵的奇异值进行裁剪。

Result: SingularClip在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的多个任务上均优于基线方法，表现出较强的性能。

Conclusion: 权重矩阵奇异值各向异性是导致神经网络可塑性损失的重要原因，SingularClip通过裁剪奇异值有效缓解了该问题。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on nonstationary tasks frequently lose the ability to fit new targets, a phenomenon referred to as loss of plasticity. We identify a novel source of plasticity loss due to the growing anisotropy of weight matrices' singular values during training, and analyze this phenomenon both empirically and theoretically. To mitigate this issue, we introduce SingularClip, a procedure that periodically clips the singular values of all weight matrices. We show that SingularClip performs strongly against baselines across a range of tasks in both continual supervised learning and deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [32] [When Does Dynamic Ensembling Pay Off? Diagnosing Regionwise Gains in Regression under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18330)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出$\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$，用少量有标注的目标域探针样本估计"区域级信任重分配"（即动态回归模型池的差异性凸组合）相对最佳静态凸组合的可实现增益。在12组数据集-漂移配对中，估计值与真实区域级测试增益的Spearman相关系数达+0.98，并通过验证门控策略（Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector）实现安全部署，同时发布OpenRegShift基准工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入依赖的"动态"回归模型池组合是否优于最佳静态混合，取决于分布漂移的性质，且部署前难以预知。作者希望利用小规模有标注的探针数据集，判断"在输入空间不同区域重新分配信任"是否值得，从而避免盲目选择动态或静态组合策略。

Method: 提出了交叉拟合增益估计量$\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$，从探针数据估算区域级凸组合相对最佳静态凸组合的增益。通过12组数据集-漂移配对及16组敏感性分析验证其预测能力，并设计了Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector门控机制，仅在置信下界超过静态基线时才部署动态模型，同时发布了OpenRegShift可复现评测框架。

Result: $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$在12组配对中预测区域级测试增益的Spearman相关系数为+0.98（95% CI [+0.83, +1.00]），敏感性分析中为+0.83，优于替代诊断指标（最高+0.66）。在前瞻性预注册批次中，所有12次运行均匹配或改善静态基线，两次部署分别降低测试风险11%和16%，门控机制成功拒绝了会导致>30倍静态损失的候选模型。

Conclusion: 小规模探针数据足以可靠地预测区域级动态组合增益，且通过下置信界门控机制可在分布漂移下安全选择动态或静态模型。动态增益源于漂移异质性与局部能力的交互，随漂移强度增大而增加，在128至256个探针标签时可实现。

Abstract: Whether input-dependent ("dynamic") combination of a regression model pool beats the best static blend depends on the shift and is rarely known before deployment. Can a small labeled target-domain probe tell us when reallocating trust across regions of the input space will pay off? We answer this with $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$, which estimates from the probe the cross-fitted gain of the regionwise convex combination over the best static convex blend: the realizable value of deciding, region by region, whom to trust. Across a frozen suite of 12 dataset-shift pairs (spatial, temporal, domain, feature-cluster), $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ predicts realized regionwise test gains with dataset-level Spearman $+0.98$ (95% CI $[+0.83, +1.00]$; $p=5\times10^{-5}$), including two cases overturning preregistered expectations. The relationship holds in a 16-pair sensitivity analysis (Spearman $+0.83$), whereas alternative probe diagnostics reach at most $+0.66$. This contrast isolates regional trust reallocation: correlation is $+0.98$ for regionwise-convex gain, but $+0.01$ for smooth covariate-dependent stacking after affine correction. A controlled generator shows dynamic gains arise from the interaction of shift heterogeneity and local competence, increase with shift severity, and become realizable between 128 and 256 probe labels in the tested grid. The Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector chooses among a static affine stacker and dynamic realizers, deploying a candidate only when a held-out lower confidence bound clears the static-convex floor. In a preregistered prospective batch, it matched or improved the floor in all 12 runs; two deployments reduced test risk by 11% and 16%, while the gate rejected a candidate whose un-gated deployment incurred $>30\times$ the static loss. We release OpenRegShift, a reproducible evaluation harness for regression ensembles under distribution shift.

</details>


### [33] [Selection, Recombination, or a Fresh Solve? A Candidate-Free Control for Single-Pass Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18379)
*Guiv Farmanfarmaian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了测试时推理中的候选聚合问题：当所有候选答案都错误时，正确候选选择不可用，但聚合仍可能给出正确答案（通过重组或重新求解）。作者引入了缺失的"无候选对照组"（相同最大输出token预算），并跨AIME-2025和HMMT-2025基准、使用Qwen3-4B进行分层分析。结果显示：多候选正确时条件化提升准确率（+0.290），全部候选错误时反而降低准确率（-0.123），单候选正确时结果不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测试时推理中，候选聚合（candidate aggregation）被广泛使用，但现有研究缺乏一个关键的对照：如果完全不使用候选，仅用同等生成预算重新求解，效果如何？这一"候选无关对照"对于判断候选上下文是否真的带来额外价值至关重要。论文旨在厘清候选条件化在不同正确候选数量下的真实贡献，避免将"额外生成pass"的收益误归因于候选信息本身。

Method: 论文采用反事实设计：引入候选无关（candidate-free）对照组，在相同的最大输出token预算下进行生成，按正确候选数量（c0/c1/c2+）进行分层比较。使用AIME-2025和HMMT-2025两个数学基准，模型为Qwen3-4B，采用单次提示聚合。此外还设计了结构化干预实验（显式答案字段/掩码）以及原始格式匹配和安慰剂（placebo）测试来描述失败模式。

Result: 主要结果：1) 多候选正确时（c2+），候选条件化提升准确率+0.290；2) 全部候选错误时（c0），候选条件化反而降低准确率-0.123（即在全错候选池上条件化不如重新求解）；3) 单候选正确时（c1）结果不明确；4) c2+和c0的结论在经过自适应双基准程序保守校正后仍然成立；5) 结构化干预显示显式答案字段因果性地引导输出朝向其值，但掩码无测量到的准确率提升，且与原始格式等价性未建立。

Conclusion: 在Qwen3-4B规模下，候选聚合的价值高度取决于候选池的质量：当有多个正确候选时可以显著提升，但全部候选错误时条件化反而有害于准确率（相较于重新求解）。这意味着"全错恢复"现象可能被高估——它更可能是额外生成pass的副产品而非候选上下文的功劳。证据局限于单一模型族、两个数学基准、首答截断的候选片段和单次提示聚合，泛化性有待验证。

Abstract: When every candidate is wrong, correct-candidate selection is unavailable, yet the aggregation call can still solve the problem afresh. A correct aggregate answer may therefore reflect recombination, fresh solving, or both. For efficient test-time reasoning, the relevant question is whether candidate context adds value beyond the additional generation pass. We introduce the missing candidate-free control under the same maximum output-token allowance and stratify by the number of correct candidates. Across AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025 with Qwen3-4B, candidate conditioning improves accuracy when multiple candidates are correct ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c2+) = +0.290), lowers accuracy when every candidate is wrong ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c0) = -0.123), and remains unresolved in the one-correct regime. The c2+ and c0 conclusions survive a conservative correction for the adaptive two-benchmark procedure. Under this counterfactual, the interpretation of all-wrong recovery reverses at this scale: conditioning on an all-wrong candidate pool lowers accuracy relative to a fresh solve. Original-format matching and placebo results characterize the failures descriptively but leave their mechanism unresolved. Within a separate structured intervention, explicit answer fields causally steer outputs toward their values; masking yields no measurable accuracy improvement, and equivalence with the original format was not established. The evidence is limited to one Qwen3-4B family, two mathematics benchmarks, first-answer-truncated candidate fragments, and single-pass prompted aggregation.

</details>
