Method: 首先通过混合指数核的有限维马尔可夫化过程逼近多元Hawkes过程，并证明该逼近在Hawkes过程、强度以及原问题值函数层面的收敛性；随后在马尔可夫化逼近模型上提出连续时间确定性策略梯度算法（Hawkes-CT DDPG），该算法仅需观测事件时间、SDE解的实现和选定的衰减滤波器，无需知晓Hawkes核系数，属于模型无关方法。

Result: 在指数核、Erlang核和幂律核三种类型下，将所提出的Hawkes-CT DDPG连续时间强化学习方法与离散时间强化学习技术进行了系统比较，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有路径依赖记忆的非马尔可夫Hawkes驱动控制问题提供了一套理论保证的马尔可夫化逼近框架和实用的模型无关连续时间强化学习求解算法。

Abstract: We study stochastic control of multivariate Hawkes-driven stochastic differential equations with machine learning algorithms in a non-Markovian setting. Due to the path dependence of the memory of the Hawkes intensity, this problem does not fall within classical stochastic control theory outside particular Markovian kernels. We first develop a finite-dimensional Markovianization procedure and algorithm to approximate multivariate Hawkes processes with mixtures of exponential kernels. We prove the convergence of the Markovianized approximation of the Hawkes process, its intensity, and the value of the problem to the original non-Markovian processes and the value of the primal problem. We then formulate continuous-time deterministic policy gradient learning on the Markovianized approximation of the problem, called Hawkes-CT DDPG. We propose a model-free algorithm to solve the non-Markovian Hawkes-driven optimization by observing only the event times of the process, the realization of the solution to the SDE, and a chosen set of decay filters, while the Hawkes kernel coefficients remain unknown. We compare our continuous time reinforcement learning Hawkes-CT DDPG method with discrete time reinforcement learning techniques under three different types of kernels: simple exponential, Erlang, and power-law kernels.

</details>


### [104] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过24次反事实实验（32个GPT-2 124M模型），精确测量单个训练样本对模型的影响。结果显示，样本在一次暴露后被学习但随后衰减；在训练结束时，暴露与未暴露样本的模型几乎无法区分，但权重位移达种子间距离的44.1%，说明样本仅将模型在损失盆地内部搬移，而未改变其最终状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献通常只能估计而无法精确测量，因为精确测量需要两次昂贵的完整预训练（仅在一个batch的一行上不同）。作者希望通过小规模反事实实验，真正测量而非估计单个样本的影响。

Method: 在OpenWebText上从头训练32个GPT-2 124M参数模型，分为四组条件×八种随机种子。在训练第200步（峰值学习率）时，将256行batch中的一行替换为固定上下文中注入的194-token段落。四个条件为：流畅散文（语料库真实主体）、流畅散文（虚构主体，与前者在全批量梯度增量上匹配度差异<0.14%）、随机键盘字符、以及无注入的孪生对照组。测量包括交叉熵、插值损失障碍、逐层中心核对齐、权重位移等多种几何指标。

Result: 注入后50步，暴露段落的模型比未暴露的模型在该段落的交叉熵上低0.039和0.044 nats（8/8种子，p<10⁻⁴）。但最终步骤检测不到此差异（p=0.25和0.71），两种段落之间也无显著差异（p=0.54）。插值损失障碍对比为+0.0068（p=0.509），持有交叉熵差异为-0.00044（p=0.310）。权重位移达到种子间欧氏距离的44.1%，且在中点已92%稳定，而损失障碍仅为种子间障碍的3.0%，两者相差约15倍。

Conclusion: 单个训练样本对模型的影响是短暂的——在训练后期会完全衰减。尽管该样本造成了显著的权重位移（占种子间差异的近一半），但模型仍在同一损失盆地内移动，未跨出盆地边界。这为理解单个样本对最终模型几乎没有持久影响提供了直接的反事实证据。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [105] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lévy Attention，一种用于不规则采样时间序列的交叉注意力算子，通过将注意力层形式化为对非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分，在预测的同时以闭合形式、零额外成本输出每个答案的信任度（不确定性）。该方法在期望上等价于软化的余弦核注意力，可替代softmax层并支持精确梯度训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度模型可以回答任意连续时间戳上的查询，但无法报告每个答案的信任程度，即缺乏不确定性量化能力。

Method: 将交叉注意力重新表述为对非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分：查询-键兼容性在连续(时间×通道)索引空间上组装强度，测度在其下散射原子，输出对这些原子处的插值值场取平均。期望上等价于软化余弦核注意力，并保留softmax丢弃的证据量Λ_q和值离散度trΣ_V(q)为闭合形式，通过精确方差恒等式组合成不确定性估计σ̂(q)。

Result: 在t-PatchGNN上，算子替换最多损失5.6%精度（稀疏数据集上无损失）；免费的不确定性信号优于20次MC dropout；σ̂缩放的校准高斯在零样本CRPS上优于50次采样器；分裂共形包装达到每级名义覆盖率；单次pass在1.4秒内对3,383个未见患者进行信任排名。

Conclusion: 注意力层本身可以通过正确的随机化形式来同时提供预测和不确定性估计，离散度携带主要信号，而证据因子在密集数据上无信息、在稀疏数据上强信息，实现了零额外成本的可靠不确定性量化。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GC-OPD（组校准在线蒸馏）方法，解决长上下文任务中在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的token级教师信号与任务级验证器奖励不一致的问题。通过分组标准化验证器奖励与轨迹级OPD分数，计算符号化差异残差并结合相对优势信用分配（RACA）逐token分配，在五个长上下文基准上显著提升Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文任务中，token级教师信号可能偏向局部合理的回答，而遗漏跨输入分布的证据或违反全局任务约束；任务级验证器虽能评估整体完成度，但两者在长输入范围内存在系统性不一致，需要弥合这一差距。

Method: GC-OPD在一个rollout组内分别对验证器奖励和轨迹级OPD分数进行归一化，计算两者的差作为符号化的教师-验证器不一致残差；RACA根据token的相对OPD优势将该轨迹级残差分配到各token，同时保留原有OPD信号。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准上，Qwen3-4B平均分从29.08提升至40.47（vanilla OPD为39.31），Qwen3-8B从35.12提升至44.65（vanilla OPD为43.56）。消融实验证明符号化残差优于额外OPD项或直接组归一化验证器奖励，RACA优于均匀token分配。

Conclusion: 组相对残差校准可以在不丢弃密集token级指导的前提下有效整合验证器结果，是长上下文蒸馏中调和教师信号与验证器反馈的有效方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [Position: Collusion Risks Among AI Reasoning Agents Justify Certification Requirements for Making Market Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18078)
*Matthew Riemer,Tommaso Tosato,Amin Memarian,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Glen Berseth,Irina Rish,Guillaume Dumas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了具备思维链推理能力的AI代理倾向于表现出合谋行为，因此在进行影响经济市场的决策前必须获得行为认证。实验表明DeepSeek-R1代理在Bertrand寡头定价领域表现出默契合谋倾向，且其思维链可被引导至极端合谋或高度竞争行为而难以被语义检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将推理型AI代理融入社会可能导致法律上区分竞争与合谋的证据标准崩溃，同时又不削弱经济损害上的区分。这是现有监管框架面临的新挑战。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1代理在Bertrand寡头定价领域进行实验，测试其是否表现出合谋行为，并验证思维链是否可被操纵且难以通过另一LLM进行语义检测。

Result: 实验发现代理表现出即使人类提示不要合谋也持续存在的默契合谋倾向；思维链可被引导至极端合谋或高度竞争行为，且该引导方式无法被另一LLM语义检测到。

Conclusion: 部署推理代理进行市场决策将导致合谋的经济后果，但缺乏共谋或意图的证据。因此需要基于代表性情境中观察到的行为进行认证，初步证据显示代理可被引导向高效竞争均衡，但全面行为认证仍需开发。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI agents with chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities are predisposed to exhibit collusive behavior and should be required to obtain behavioral certification before making decisions that affect economic markets. This is because integrating these agents into society could collapse the legal evidentiary distinction between competition and collusion among independent firms without eroding the economic harm distinction. Experiments with DeepSeek-R1 agents in the Bertrand oligopoly pricing domain reveal a tendency towards tacit collusion that persists even when humans prompt the agents not to collude. We further show that the chain-of-thought of these agents can be steered toward either extremely collusive or highly competitive behavior in a way that is not semantically detectable by another LLM analyzing the reasoning traces. As a result, deploying reasoning agents for market decisions leads to collusive economic outcomes without any evidence of conspiracy or intent. Thus, certification based on observed behavior in representative situations is necessary to prevent collusion. We provide preliminary evidence that such agents can be steered in a generalizable way toward efficient competitive equilibria. However, developing a comprehensive behavioral certification will be required before these models can be deployed in real-world markets while ensuring their stability and efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [Position: Profiling Game Worlds by Transition Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18079)
*Lele Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出过渡复杂度剖面（TCP），一套标准化、可复现的指标集，用于刻画游戏环境或游戏数据集的转移核复杂度，涵盖一步分支、交互不确定性、时间/空间依赖跨度，并呼吁将其作为GWM和RL论文的标准基准元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏世界建模（GWM）与强化学习（RL）经常被混淆，因为研究论文很少量化在给定接口（像素/令牌/潜在表征及其有限历史）下潜在转移预测问题的难度，导致环境复杂度缺乏可比性。

Method: 提出Transition Complexity Profile (TCP)，包含三个维度的度量：(i) 内在一步分支度；(ii) 交互引发的不确定性与可观测对手影响；(iii) 基于标准化探针曲线的时间/空间依赖跨度。TCP附带明确的参考分布、协议随机性和版本化测量预算（采样/重采样与固定探针计算量），确保跨基准可比。

Result: 论文概述了常见游戏家族和现代"神经游戏引擎"领域在该复杂度景观中的分布，并说明TCP作为基准元数据的适用性。

Conclusion: 呼吁将TCP确立为GWM和RL论文中的标准基准元数据及必报统计量，以提升环境复杂度报告的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Game world modeling (GWM) and reinforcement learning (RL) are often confounded because research papers rarely quantify how difficult the underlying transition prediction problem is at the declared interface (pixels/tokens/latents with finite history). We propose the Transition Complexity Profile (TCP): a small, reproducible set of metrics that characterizes an environment's (or gameplay dataset's) induced transition kernel by (i) intrinsic one-step branching, (ii) interaction-induced uncertainty and opponent influence when observable, and (iii) temporal/spatial dependency span via standardized probe curves. TCP is reported with an explicit reference distribution, protocol stochasticity, and a versioned measurement budget (sampling/resampling and fixed probe compute), enabling comparable numbers across benchmarks. We outline how common game families and modern "neural game engine" domains populate this landscape and call for TCP to become standard benchmark metadata and a required statistic in GWM and RL papers.

</details>


### [109] [Large Language Models in Mental Health: A Systematic Review of Applications, Innovations, and Ethical Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18080)
*Yisong Chen,Yifan Gao,Sijing Yu,Chuqing Zhao,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了大语言模型（LLMs）在心理健康领域的应用，涵盖社交媒体分析、临床对话代理、治疗支持工具、提示工程、多模态学习及伦理考量等方面，整合多学科研究以支持抑郁早期检测、自杀风险评估和个性化治疗，并强调安全、公平、负责任的部署框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康问题日益严峻，而大语言模型展现出了在心理健康领域的巨大潜力。然而，该领域的研究分散且跨学科，缺乏系统性的综述来整合最新进展、方法论和挑战。作者旨在通过全面回顾LLMs在心理健康中的应用现状，指导未来的研究方向并推动安全可靠的落地。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统整合了跨学科研究，涵盖多种数据源（社交媒体帖子、电子病历、多模态输入），从多个维度进行分析：（1）LLMs在各类心理健康任务中的应用（抑郁检测、自杀风险评估、个性化治疗支持、心理教育内容生成）；（2）模型架构与标注策略的进展；（3）提示工程对领域适配的关键作用；（4）多模态融合技术（文本、语音、传感器数据）；（5）伦理、社会技术及监管挑战的分析。

Result: 综述识别了LLMs在心理健康各应用方向上的重要进展，包括增强可解释性和临床相关性的模型与标注策略，提示工程技术对领域适配的促进作用，以及多模态融合方法对心理健康诊断和监测的改善。同时指出现有应用仍面临伦理、社会技术和监管层面的持续挑战，亟需建立全面的框架来保障部署的安全性、公平性和问责性。

Conclusion: LLMs在心理健康领域具有广阔的应用前景，能够改善早期检测、风险评估和个性化干预。然而，要实现在真实世界医疗环境中的安全部署，需要建立涵盖伦理、社会技术和监管维度的综合框架，确保LLMs的应用是安全、公平且可问责的。未来的研究应继续推进多模态融合和领域适配技术，同时加强伦理治理。

Abstract: We present a review on the applications of large language models (LLMs) in health, e.g., social media analysis, clinical conversational agents, therapy support tools, prompt engineering, multimodal learning, and ethical considerations. We integrate findings from interdisciplinary studies utilizing diverse data sources such as social media posts, electronic medical records, and multimodal inputs to enable early detection of depression, suicide risk assessment, personalized therapy support, and psychoeducational content generation. Our review highlights advancements in LLM models and annotation strategies that enhance interpretability and clinical relevance, while we also emphasize the critical role of prompt engineering for domain adaptation. We also discuss emerging multimodal fusion techniques integrating text, speech, and sensor data for improved mental health diagnosis and monitoring. Finally, we address ongoing ethical, sociotechnical, and regulatory challenges, and advocate frameworks to ensure safe, equitable, and accountable deployment of LLMs in real-world mental health care.

</details>


### [110] [Position: Behavioral Systems Require Behavioral Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18081)
*Manuel Cherep,Nikhil Singh,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张AI智能体应像其他行为系统一样被评估，即通过系统性的观察、扰动和解释其行为过程，而非仅仅关注性能结果，并提出了一套发展严谨行为测试的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体的评估方法过度聚焦于性能产出（performance outcomes），忽略了产生这些结果背后的行为过程。然而，AI智能体日益作为行为系统运行——与动态环境交互、追求目标并随时间适应，因此需要借鉴行为科学的方法来全面评估其行为。

Method: 论文从行为科学中汲取经验教训，提出构建严谨行为测试的研究议程，具体包括三个方向：(1)从动作序列中恢复决策策略；(2)构建能够隔离行为差异的环境；(3)探测多智能体系统中的涌现动力学。

Result: 论文提出了一条发展"AI行为科学"的路线图，为AI智能体的行为评估提供了系统化的方法论框架。

Conclusion: AI智能体应被视为行为系统进行评估，通过系统观察、扰动和解释其行为过程来理解其内在机制，这将为AI评估开辟新的研究方向。

Abstract: Artificial agentic systems increasingly operate as behavioral systems by interacting with dynamic environments, pursuing goals, and adapting over time. Yet, current evaluation methods largely focus on performance outcomes, not the underlying behavioral processes that produce them. This paper argues that AI agents must be evaluated like other behavioral systems: through systematic observation, perturbation, and interpretation of their actions. We draw on lessons from the behavioral sciences to motivate this position, and propose a research agenda focused on developing rigorous behavioral tests. These include methods for recovering decision strategies from action sequences, constructing environments that isolate behavioral differences, and probing emergent dynamics in multi-agent systems. Taken together, these directions offer a roadmap for developing a science of AI behavior.

</details>


### [111] [Position: Current Model Cards Are Insufficient for Downstream Governance of Open-Weight Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18086)
*Sungwon Chae,Keonwoo Kim,Hoki Kim,Jaeyeon Ju,Sangchul Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于对Hugging Face上500个模型卡片的分析，提出开放权重基础模型（OWFM）的有效治理需要整合模型卡片、可接受使用政策（AUP）和许可证三层互补组件，以弥合现有监管方法的安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重基础模型（OWFM）的增长促使AI社区重新评估下游治理策略。虽然模型卡片已被广泛采用，但现有框架未能充分告知下游开发者和用户关于OWFM特有的安全挑战，存在治理上的安全缺口。

Method: 本文为立场论文，采用实证分析与论证相结合的方法：分析了Hugging Face平台上500个模型卡片，从模型传承（heritage）、对齐溯源（alignment provenance）和经验观察行为等维度识别现有监管方法遗留的安全缺口，并论证标准开源许可证（OSL）不适合OWFM及其对AUP可执行性的削弱影响。

Result: 研究发现现有模型卡片在安全关键信息方面存在明显缺口；标准开源许可证不适用于OWFM并可能削弱AUP的可执行性；据此提出模型卡片、AUP和许可证应演化为综合安全工件的方向。

Conclusion: 有效的OWFM治理需要将信息、规范和法律三个维度协调整合，将模型卡片、AUP和许可证发展为一体化的安全工件，构建更全面的治理框架。

Abstract: The growth of open-weight foundation models (OWFMs) has prompted the AI community to re-evaluate strategies for effective downstream governance. Although model cards have been widely adopted as transparency artifacts in model repositories, existing frameworks often fail to adequately inform downstream developers and users about the distinct safety challenges posed by OWFMs. This position paper analyzes 500 model cards hosted on Hugging Face and argues that effective governance of OWFMs requires a multi-layered approach integrating three complementary components: (i) model cards, (ii) acceptable use policies (AUPs), and (iii) licenses. To motivate this claim, we identify a safety gap left by existing regulatory approaches, including model heritage, alignment provenance, and empirically observed behaviors, through an analysis of model cards with safety-critical information. We further argue that standard open-source licenses (OSLs) are not well suited for OWFMs and may weaken the enforceability of AUPs. Building on these observations, we outline directions for evolving model cards, AUPs, and licenses into integrated safety artifacts to enable a more comprehensive governance framework that coherently integrates informational, normative, and legal dimensions.

</details>


### [112] [A Metamorphic Artificial Age Score Decision-Support Prototype for Flight-Log-Based Drone Propeller Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18088)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变质人造年龄分数（AAS）的决策支持原型，利用飞行日志数据对无人机螺旋桨故障进行健康监测。通过六个健康指标（轨迹跟踪误差、姿态不稳定、推力命令负担、电机命令失衡、ESC命令不稳定和电池水平压力）对历史飞行数据进行评估，并结合冗余调整的AAS公式和变质充分性关系进行分类。在2024 DronePropA公开数据集上，对四种案例（一健康、三重度缺陷）进行回顾性评估，结果显示不同严重程度的螺旋桨故障会在不同操作通道表现，支持多指标决策层方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机螺旋桨故障的影响分散在多个飞行日志通道中，而非表现为单一诊断信号，这给安全和可靠性带来了风险。现有方法难以从多通道综合识别故障模式并优先安排维护，因此需要一种多指标决策支持工具来实现基于飞行日志的螺旋桨健康监测和维护优先级排序。

Method: 利用2024 DronePropA公开数据集的历史真实飞行日志，从原始MATLAB矩阵中计算六个健康相关指标，相对健康基线归一化后，通过候选评分策略、变质充分性关系和冗余调整的AAS公式进行评估。其中AAS作为结构性策略充分性和负担度量（而非时间年龄度量）。针对一个健康基线和三个缺陷螺旋桨案例（相同速度剖面和轨迹）进行受控回顾性评估。

Result: 健康案例被分配到常规监测；严重度1案例以ESC命令不稳定为主，分配到维护审查；严重度2案例达到最大电机命令和ESC命令负担，严重度3案例达到最大轨迹跟踪误差，两者均触发强制检查。结果表明螺旋桨故障效应可能通过不同操作通道显现。

Conclusion: 不同严重程度的螺旋桨故障通过不同操作通道表现，验证了多指标决策支持层对飞行后维护优先级排序和自主系统监督的必要性。

Abstract: Drone propeller faults can create safety and reliability risks when their effects are distributed across multiple flight-log channels rather than appearing as a single diagnostic signal. This paper proposes a Metamorphic Artificial Age Score (AAS) decision-support prototype for flight-log-based drone propeller health monitoring. Using selected historical real flight logs from the 2024 DronePropA public dataset, the framework computes six health-related indicators from raw MATLAB matrices: trajectory tracking error, attitude instability, thrust-command burden, motor-command imbalance, ESC-command instability, and battery-level stress. These indicators are normalized relative to a healthy baseline and evaluated through candidate scoring policies, metamorphic adequacy relations, and a redundancy-adjusted AAS formulation. In this context, AAS is used as a structural policy-adequacy and burden measure rather than as a chronological age measure. A controlled retrospective evaluation was performed using one healthy baseline and three defective propeller cases under the same speed profile and trajectory. The healthy case was assigned to routine monitoring. The Severity 1 case was dominated by ESC-command instability and assigned to maintenance review. The Severity 2 case reached maximum motor-command and ESC-command burden, while the Severity 3 case reached maximum trajectory tracking error; both triggered mandatory inspection. The results show that propeller fault effects may appear through different operational channels, supporting the need for a multi-indicator decision-support layer for post-flight maintenance prioritization and autonomous-system oversight.

</details>


### [113] [Position: Multi-Agent Systems Should Prioritize Concurrency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18092)
*Xin Yang,Letian Li,Zimo Ji,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Wenyuan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种观点：基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中的许多失败本质上属于并发控制问题，并主张将并发控制作为MAS框架的一等设计关注点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中的并发读写共享状态会导致陈旧读取、丢失更新和不一致结果，特别是LLM长推理窗口放大了这些风险，而现有框架往往忽视了这一问题。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）形式，将MAS中的失败模式映射到经典并发控制异常，并提出通过冲突检测、隔离保证和结构化共享资源访问等显式并发控制机制来缓解。

Result: 论证了MAS失败可系统性地归因于并发控制问题，而非传统上认为的协调或通信故障，并倡导并发控制应成为MAS框架中的首要设计考量。

Conclusion: 并发控制不应是事后补救，而应是MAS框架的核心设计原则，以实现扩展性的同时保持可靠性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) promise scalable collaboration, yet adding agents often reduces reliability. This position paper argues that many MAS failures are fundamentally concurrency control problems: agents concurrently read and write shared state, and long LLM inference windows amplify the risk of stale reads, lost updates, and inconsistent outcomes. Failure modes commonly attributed to coordination or communication breakdowns can be mapped directly onto classical concurrency anomalies. We contend that MAS frameworks should address these failures through explicit concurrency control mechanisms: conflict detection, isolation guarantees, and structured access to shared resources. Concurrency control should be a first-class design concern, not an afterthought.

</details>


### [114] [FinSkillBench: Evaluating AI Agents and Domain Skills for Investment Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18099)
*Jermyn Zhen Yong Bek,Zhuang Qiang Bok,Zhongtian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinSkillBench，一个用于评估语言模型智能体在投资管理领域使用金融专业技能能力的评测套件，涵盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三大领域共12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。实验发现精心策划的技能包能显著提升性能（平均分从0.366提升至0.528），而智能体自我生成的技能几乎无增益且计算成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资管理是高风险的领域，智能体AI系统不能仅生成看似合理的文本，还必须检索时点数据、组装正确的计算输入、调用专门方法并产出可审计的结构化输出。现有评测缺乏对金融领域技能有效使用的系统评估，因此需要专门的基准来衡量语言模型智能体在真实投资管理任务中的表现。

Method: 构建FinSkillBench评测套件，覆盖投资组合构建、风险管理和基本面分析三个领域，包含12个子任务和2,603个任务片段。每个片段提供时点输入、隐藏真值和任务特定验证器。比较三种条件：无技能、精心策划的技能包（包含程序文档和可执行组件）、自我生成技能（智能体在片段内自行编写和复用程序）。在9个模型上进行大规模评估，并使用独立框架Hermes Agent（8个模型，5,280个片段）进行复现验证。

Result: 精心策划的技能包持续改善所有模型的表现，平均分从0.366提升到0.528，在投资组合构建和风险管理领域收益最大。自我生成技能几乎无增益且计算成本更高。独立评估（Hermes Agent框架，8模型，5,280片段）在三个领域复现了相同的方向性模式，但技能效果的幅度随子任务和框架不同而变化。

Conclusion: 在投资管理智能体中，获取可靠的程序性技能与模型选择同样重要，而天真的自我生成技能往往无效。作者公开了基准、评估工具、策划的技能包和完整轨迹以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Investment management is a high-stakes domain in which agentic AI systems must do more than generate plausible text. They must retrieve point-in-time data, assemble correct computational inputs, invoke specialized methods, and produce auditable structured outputs. We introduce FinSkillBench, an evaluation suite designed to measure whether language model agents can effectively use financial domain skills to solve investment management tasks. The benchmark spans three domains, portfolio construction, risk management, and fundamental analysis, and includes 12 subtasks with 2,603 task episodes.
  Each episode provides point-in-time inputs, hidden ground truth, and a task-specific verifier.We compare three conditions: no skill, curated skill packages consisting of procedural documents and executable components, and self-generated skills in which the agent writes and reuses its own procedures within an episode. Across 9 models and a large-scale evaluation, curated skills consistently improve performance, raising mean scores from 0.366 to 0.528, with the largest gains in portfolio construction and risk management.
  In contrast, self-generated skills provide little benefit despite higher computational cost. An independent evaluation using a separate agent framework (Hermes Agent, 8 models, 5,280 episodes total) reproduces the directional pattern across all three domains, with the magnitude of skill effects varying by subtask and harness.
  These results showthat in investment management agents, access to reliable procedural skills can be as important as model choice, while naive self-generation of skills is often ineffective. We release the benchmark, evaluation tools, curated skill packages, and full trajectories to support further research.

</details>


### [115] [Self-Evolving Agents as Dynamic Graph Transformation: A Survey and New Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18104)
*Yuanyuan Xu,Wenjie Zhang,Yin Chen,Xuemin Lin,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体演化视为动态图变换的框架，将智能体状态建模为动态图（记忆、工具、技能、工作流和智能体间关系作为类型化节点/边/子图），并以此为核心组织现有方法，构建了包含节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活和跨组件协同演化四类方法的分类体系，同时提出动态图学习作为智能体演化的可复用基础设施，并讨论了图感知的评估与治理协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体正成为自我演化的系统，其状态具有结构性和动态性，但现有调查中，图仅被视为智能体功能的支撑结构，而非演化的基底；自我演化智能体的调查又聚焦智能体级机制，很少讨论图拓扑演化。演化智能体状态与动态图拓扑之间的耦合关系尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将"智能体演化"形式化为"动态图变换"：智能体状态建模为动态图，通过模式约束的重写操作来更新类型化节点、边和子图。在此基础上，将基于动态图的自演化智能体方法组织为四种分类：节点/特征演化、边/拓扑演化、子图激活、跨组件协同演化，并据此提出动态图学习作为可复用基础设施，映射九个动态图学习子领域到智能体演化能力。

Result: 构建了四种分类体系以组织现有基于动态图的自演化智能体方法，映射了九个动态图学习子领域到智能体演化能力并讨论其适应性与可能的失败模式，提出了五种从动态图视角出发的图感知评估与治理协议。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为设计和治理自演化智能体提供一个紧凑的结构化视角（透镜），弥合图智能体与自我演化智能体两条研究线之间的鸿沟，该工作仍是一个综述性探讨，目标是为该领域提供系统性框架而非具体方法实现。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly becoming self-evolving systems that persist across interactions, maintain memories, use tools, acquire skills, refine workflows, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make agent states structural and dynamic: entities, relations, attributes, dependencies, and execution structures change with new evidence, feedback, and environmental conditions. Existing graph-agent surveys typically treat graphs as support structures for agent functions rather than as evolving substrates, while self-evolving-agent surveys focus on agent-level mechanisms and rarely discuss graph topology evolution. Thus, the coupling between evolving agent state and dynamic graph topology remains underexplored. This survey connects these two research lines by framing \textit{agent evolution as dynamic graph transformation}. We model agent state as a dynamic graph, where memories, tools, skills, workflows, and inter-agent relations are represented as typed nodes, edges, and subgraphs updated through schema-constrained rewrites. Based on this formulation, we organize existing dynamic-graph-based methods for self-evolving agents into four taxonomies: node/feature evolution, edge/topology evolution, subgraph activation, and cross-component co-evolution. Building on this taxonomy, we propose dynamic graph learning as reusable infrastructure for self-evolving agents and map nine dynamic-graph-learning subfields to agent-evolution capabilities, discussing their adaptations and possible failure modes. Finally, we discuss five types of graph-aware evaluation and governance protocols from a dynamic-graph perspective, which complement end-task evaluation. The goal is to provide a compact structural lens for designing and governing self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [116] [Emergence of Agentic AI: A Review on Evolution, Background, Working Principles, Applications, Adoption Factors, and Future Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18110)
*AKM Bahalul Haque,Al Amin Islam Ridoy,Mohammad Rayhan,Ivan Porres*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对Agentic AI（智能体人工智能）进行了系统性综述，涵盖其基础原理、历史理论演变、架构与功能、跨领域实际应用、当前挑战及未来研究方向，并提出了一个包含系统质量维度的利益相关者采纳意图框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI在人工智能领域快速发展并展现出变革潜力，但学界和业界对其缺乏深入理解和全面把握。为了评估其改进空间和应用前景，需要对该领域的最新研究现状进行系统性梳理和综述。

Method: 采用系统性综述的方法，收集并分析近年发表的Agentic AI相关学术文献，涉及多个领域。综述内容包括：Agentic AI的基础原理与工作机制、人工智能系统中代理性的历史与理论演变、架构与功能探索、跨领域实际应用案例、研究结果分析、当前挑战识别以及未来研究方向的讨论。此外，还提出了一套利益相关者采纳意图的综合框架，并引入了系统质量维度。

Result: 该综述为研究者和实践者提供了对Agentic AI的全面理解，包括其当前发展状况与应用情况，识别了关键研究空白，并勾勒了未来研究方向。同时提出了基于系统质量维度的Agentic AI采纳意图框架。

Conclusion: 本文通过系统性综述为Agentic AI领域提供了全面的知识图谱，帮助研究者和实践者理解该技术的现状、潜力和挑战，并为未来的研究与实践指明了方向。

Abstract: Agentic AI is gaining new insights and advancements in the field of Artificial Intelligence, fostering significant potential to enable rapid transformation across various domains.This rapid advancement and the potential to revolutionize various domains advocate the need for a deeper understanding and firm grasp of the technology. Moreover, an investigation into state of the art research directions in agentic AI needs to be conducted to comprehensively assess the potential scope for improvement and application.Therefore, to address these objectives, a comprehensive review can provide researchers and practitioners with valuable insights into the current state and future research scopes of agentic AI.Hence, this work considers the recently published scholarly contributions in agentic AI across various domains and discusses the fundamentals and working principles of Agentic AI, traces the historical and theoretical evolution of agency in artificial systems, explores and discusses Agentic AIs architecture, working principles, and functionalities, explores real-world applications of Agentic AI across various domains, analyzes the research findings, identifies current challenges, and discuss potential future research directions, and proposes a comprehensive framework of stakeholders intention to use and adopt Agentic AI with the help of proposed system quality dimensions.Therefore, this systematic review provides researchers and practitioners with a comprehensive understanding of Agentic AI, its current developments and applications, highlights key research gaps, and outlines future research directions.

</details>


### [117] [Solving Is Not Drawing: A Benchmark for Diagrammatic Reasoning in Olympiad Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18111)
*Hsien Xin Peng,Anthony Kim,Alvin Li,Calvin Supasanya,Shivank Garg,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开放源码的基准测试，包含954道奥林匹克几何题（其中297道为高难度子集），每道题配有可渲染的Asymptote代码高保真图及多维度评估指标，专门用于衡量模型"图解推理"能力。实验发现，当前基础模型在解题与绘图之间存在显著差距，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%，表明强大的数学推理能力并不等同于能构造准确的几何图形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如MathVista、MathVerse）只衡量模型能否得出正确答案，但几何问题求解与构造正确图形是两种不同技能。目前缺乏一个能单独评估模型构造几何图形能力的基准，导致这一关键能力未被测量。

Method: 构建包含954道自包含奥林匹克几何问题的开放源码基准，每个问题配有人工标注的高保真Asymptote代码图；开发了基于文本、代码、图像、VLM和约束的多维度指标来评估"图解推理"能力。

Result: 对当前基础模型的评估显示，其作图保真度明显低于解题能力，平均编译成功率仅为36.14%，揭示了解题与绘图之间存在显著的能力鸿沟。

Conclusion: 强大的数学推理能力并不等同于能够构造准确的几何图形，这两者是需要分别评估和培养的独立能力。该基准为系统衡量和提升模型的图解推理能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Foundation models such as GPT and Claude now solve olympiad-level mathematics with remarkable proficiency, so much so that geometry problem solving has become a standard proxy for their mathematical reasoning. Yet solving a geometry problem and drawing the figure it depends on are not the same skill: progress often hinges on a faithful diagram with the right auxiliary constructions and incidences, and it is unclear that a model which reasons its way to the answer can also produce one. A growing collection of benchmarks, including MathVista, and MathVerse, measures whether models reach the correct answer, but to our knowledge, none isolate the distinct ability to construct the diagram itself, leaving this capability unmeasured. We introduce an open-source benchmark that targets this gap: 954 self-contained olympiad geometry problems, with a 297-problem hard subset, each paired with its solution and a human-authored, high-fidelity diagram in renderable Asymptote code, together with a suite of text-, code-, image-, VLM-, and constraint-based metrics for what we term diagrammatic reasoning. Evaluating current foundation models reveals a pronounced gap between solving and drawing: their diagrams are markedly less faithful, with an average compile success rate of only 36.14\%. Strong mathematical reasoning, we find, does not imply the ability to construct accurate geometric diagrams. Our benchmark and dataset can be accessed at https://huggingface.co/datasets/max98765/hard_geometry_problems_with_diagrams.

</details>


### [118] [Position: AI Leaderboards Are Underserving the Global South: A Case Study from India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18117)
*Sourav Banerjee,Saikat Saha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证AI排行榜在制度设计上不适合服务全球南方，缺乏独立治理、利益冲突政策和指标演进机制。以印度为案例（与58位AI从业者咨询），指出解决方案不是更多数据，而是建立具有独立治理的区域排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI排行榜存在结构性缺陷，未能将全球南方已有的高质量区域基准（如IndicSUPERB、MILU、LAHAJA、IrokoBench、AlGhafa）纳入其中，且缺乏治理机制来推动这一改变，导致印地语、斯瓦希里语、阿拉伯语等语言使用者面临持续未解决的评估缺口。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）研究方法，包括：1) 制度分析框架审视现有AI排行榜的结构性缺陷；2) 以印度作为案例研究（14亿人口、22种官方语言、有高质量基准但缺乏可信聚合）；3) 对58位AI从业者进行咨询调研，收集对正式治理和基于披露的利益冲突管理的偏好意见。

Result: 咨询结果显示58位AI从业者一致倾向于正式治理和基于披露的利益冲突管理机制。研究确认核心障碍不是数据缺失而是制度设计问题——全球排行榜未纳入区域基准，且无治理机制强制其采纳。

Conclusion: 解决方案不是收集更多数据，而是建立更好的制度：从一开始就建立具有独立治理的区域排行榜，以服务全球南方语言社区的需求。

Abstract: This position paper argues that AI leaderboards are structurally ill-suited to serving the Global South because they lack independent governance, conflict-of-interest policies, and mechanisms for metric evolution. The barrier is not missing data; high-quality regional benchmarks already exist: IndicSUPERB, MILU, and LAHAJA for India; IrokoBench for Africa; AlGhafa for Arabic. The barrier is institutional design. Global leaderboards do not include these benchmarks, and no governance mechanism compels them to do so. Commercial pressure corrects leaderboard failures when paying customers in the Global North are affected. The Global South lacks equivalent leverage. Without governance, failures affecting Hindi, Swahili, or Arabic speakers persist indefinitely as documented but unaddressed gaps. Using India as a case study (1.4 billion people, 22 scheduled languages, high-quality benchmarks, but no trusted aggregation), we report findings from a consultation with 58 AI practitioners showing consistent preference for formal governance and disclosure-based conflict management. The solution is not more data but better institutions: regional leaderboards with independent governance from the start.

</details>


### [119] [Safety Alignment Illusion: The Cross-Lingual Safety Gap in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18131)
*Namya Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出INCLUDE基准，一个包含2,604条提示、覆盖六种印度语言的多语言评估基准，用于量化LLM中的印度文化社会文化偏见。实验评估了十个开源和闭源LLM，分析了14,988个偏见分数，发现孟加拉语在开源模型中偏见最高，而英语在开源模型中偏见最低但在闭源模型中偏见最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐训练以英语为中心，非英语语言的安全过滤器容易失效，导致语音助手和对话系统对非英语社区产生强化刻板印象的输出。对于印度语言多样性丰富的地区，这构成关键失败模式，亟需一个跨语言的基准来量化这种偏见。

Method: 构建INCLUDE多语言评估基准，包含六种提示语言（英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语、泰米尔语和印英混合语）共2,604条提示，对十个开源和闭源LLM进行评估，共分析14,988个偏见分数。

Result: 两个关键发现：一是孟加拉语在开源模型中产生最高的平均偏见分数；二是英语呈现显著逆转——在开源模型中偏见最低，但在闭源模型中偏见最高。

Conclusion: 现有的英语中心安全对齐在不同模型架构建下对各语言的安全保障不一致，非英语语言尤其需要更针对性的偏见检测和缓解策略，以保障印度多元化语言社区的用户安全。

Abstract: Current safety alignment training for Large Language Models (LLMs) are heavily English-centric. When such safety filters fail for non-English languages, the consequences are immediate and user-facing: voice assistants and spoken dialogue systems may produce stereotype-reinforcing outputs, bypassing the standard English-focused safety alignments and propagating harmful bias to non-English speaking communities. For spoken language technologies deployed across India's linguistically diverse population, this represents a critical failure mode. To address this cross-lingual gap, we introduce INCLUDE (Indian Cultural Lens for Understanding and Detecting Embedded Biases), a multilingual evaluation benchmark designed to quantify Indian-centric socio-cultural biases. INCLUDE consists of 2,604 prompts spanning six prompt languages: English, Hindi, Bengali, Marathi, Tamil, and Hinglish (Hindi-English code-mix). We evaluate ten open- and closed-source LLMs against this benchmark, analyzing 14,988 bias scores. Our statistical results reveal two key findings. First, Bengali yielded the highest average bias score in open-source models. Second, English demonstrated a notable reversal, producing the lowest bias in open-source models but the highest bias in closed-source models.

</details>


### [120] [Optimized Fuzzy Logic Approach with the IEEE Key Gas Method for Diagnosing Power Transformer Faults Using Dissolved Gas Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18133)
*Kim-Anh Nguyen,Huy Hoang Le,Ba Tu Phung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合模糊逻辑与IEEE关键气体法的变压器故障诊断模型（FL-KGM），通过精炼隶属函数、优化模糊规则集以及分离CO和CO2气体，在真实数据集上实现了98.6%的诊断准确率，显著优于传统KGM及其他模糊逻辑方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变压器故障诊断对电力系统稳定性至关重要。传统的IEEE关键气体法（KGM）在处理模糊数据和保证高诊断精度方面存在局限，需要一种更优的方法来提升故障识别和分类能力。

Method: 提出了FL-KGM增强模型，主要包括：精细化的隶属函数设计、优化的模糊规则集、创新的CO和CO2气体分离（用于消除诊断不一致性），并结合多维气体比值分析和自适应分类框架。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证，FL-KGM实现了高达98.6%的准确率，显著优于传统KGM和其他基于模糊逻辑的方法。

Conclusion: FL-KGM在推进变压器监控、实现智能故障检测和增强现代电力系统预测性维护策略方面展现出巨大潜力。

Abstract: Reliable transformer fault diagnosis is essential for maintaining power system stability. The IEEE Key Gas Method (KGM), a widely utilized approach in Dissolved Gas Analysis (DGA), exhibits limitations in addressing ambiguous data and ensuring high diagnostic accuracy. This study presents An enhanced model combining Fuzzy Logic with the IEEE Key Gas Method (FL-KGM) that introduces refined membership functions, optimized fuzzy rule sets, and a novel separation of CO and CO2 to eliminate diagnostic inconsistencies. By leveraging multidimensional gas ratio analysis and an adaptive classification framework, FL-KGM delivers superior fault identification and classification. Experimental validation utilizing real-world datasets demonstrates that FL-KGM achieves up to 98.6% accuracy, significantly outperforming KGM and other FL-based approaches. These findings elucidate the potential of FL-KGM in advancing transformer monitoring, enabling intelligent fault detection, and enhancing predictive maintenance strategies in modern power systems.

</details>


### [121] [Improving Rural Medication Safety with AI: A Scoping Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18135)
*Jeong-ah Kim,Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Daniel Terry,Maryam Rouhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过范围综述（2012-2025年，涵盖12项来自9个国家的研究），探讨人工智能技术在乡村医疗环境中减少用药错误、提升患者安全的应用与效果。研究发现AI已整合至用药管理全流程（处方、配药、给药、监测），机器学习类干预可将处方和转写错误降低34%-80%，但仍面临治理框架缺失、资金限制和临床人员抵触等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用药错误是全球医疗系统的重大威胁，造成患者伤害。乡村医疗资源匮乏，安全风险更高。引入人工智能有望提升乡村医疗的患者安全水平，但目前缺乏对AI在该特定场景下应用效果的系统性梳理。

Method: 采用范围综述方法，系统检索2012至2025年间EBSCohost、Emcare（Ovid）、MEDLINE和ProQuest Consumer Health Database等多个数据库，最终纳入来自9个国家的12项原始研究，并通过主题分析法对数据进行归纳。

Result: 研究发现AI技术已覆盖用药管理的所有阶段，归纳出四个核心主题：AI技术类型（临床决策支持系统、机器学习、自然语言处理、智能输液泵）、受影响的用药流程阶段、在减少错误和提升工作流安全性方面的效果（机器学习监测可将处方和转写错误降低34%-80%），以及乡村特有的挑战（基础设施、人员培训、系统集成、警报疲劳）。主要障碍包括治理框架缺失、资金限制和临床人员抵触。

Conclusion: AI技术在乡村医疗中提升用药安全方面具有巨大潜力，能够实现数据驱动的监测、流程自动化并提供临床决策支持，但需解决治理、资金和人员接受度等障碍才能充分发挥作用。

Abstract: Introduction: Medication errors (MEs) represent a significant threat to global healthcare systems, contributing to patient harm. Introducing artificial intelligence (AI) in rural healthcare enhances patient safety. The aim is to explore the applications and effectiveness of AI technologies in enhancing patient safety and reducing medication errors in rural health settings.
  Methods: A scoping review was conducted through a systematic literature search spanning 2012 to 2025 across multiple databases, including EBSCohost, Emcare (Ovid), MEDLINE, and the ProQuest Consumer Health Database. Twelve primary studies from nine different nations were examined. Data were analysed thematically to obtain insights on AI interventions across the medication process.
  Results: AI technologies have been integrated into every stage of medication management, right from prescribing and dispensing to administration and post-administration monitoring. Four key themes came to light: (1) the various types of AI being utilised (like Clinical Decision Support Systems, Machine Learning, Natural Language Processing, and smart pumps); (2) the phases of the medication process that are affected; (3) how effective these technologies are in minimising errors and boosting workflow safety; and (4) rural-specific challenges including infrastructure, staff training, system integration, and alert fatigue. Several studies have demonstrated that machine learning-based surveillance improves incident detection and reduces prescribing and transcription errors by an impressive 34% to 80%. Barriers included lack of governance frameworks, financial limitations, and clinician resistance, which still present major obstacles.
  Conclusion: In rural healthcare, AI technologies hold great potential for enhancing pharmaceutical safety. They can allow data-driven monitoring, automate processes, and offer clinical decision assistance.

</details>


### [122] [FraudBench: Stress-Testing Policy-Grounded Banking Agents Against Adaptive Fraud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18136)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FraudBench，一个面向银行业的可执行对抗性欺诈安全基准测试平台，基于 τ²-bench 双控框架和 τ-Knowledge 银行环境构建，包含 698 篇内部政策文档和 150 个对抗场景，用于评估政策约束下的银行智能体在面临对话式身份操纵、授权欺骗和信任操控时的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融欺诈基准仅针对静态交易或消息分类，通用智能体安全基准也只关注提示注入或一般有害用途，均无法测试政策约束下的银行智能体在对话中被呼叫者操纵身份、授权和信任时的安全行为。这是银行业中客服与授权、欺诈检测和政策合规不可分割的切实问题。

Method: 基于 τ²-bench 双控框架和 τ-Knowledge 银行环境构建可执行基准；智能体与模拟呼叫者均通过工具作用于共享可变账户状态，智能体可授予呼叫者部分工具访问权限，环境提供 698 篇内部政策文档供智能体检索。包含 150 个作者编写的对抗场景（公开 107 个用于评估，其中 90 个覆盖十种欺诈机制，17 个链式自适应攻击，另 43 个链式攻击作为保留集）。每个场景标注可观察证据、禁止行为、安全处置和干预点。

Result: 对 4 个智能体在 107 个分级任务上进行初步单次评估，攻击安全性仅在 49% 到 65% 之间，其中钱骡（money-mule）和首方欺诈（first-party fraud）是跨模型最常见的弱点。

Conclusion: FraudBench 填补了政策约束下对话式银行智能体安全性评估的空白，初步结果显示当前智能体在反欺诈安全方面存在显著不足，尤其在钱骡和首方欺诈场景中表现脆弱，需要更强大的安全机制来应对历史依赖性的链式攻击。

Abstract: Conversational agents now act for end users through tools while holding access to customer databases and internal policy documents that a caller can reach through dialogue alone. Banking is the clearest case: the same agent that answers a question can also change contact details, reset a PIN, or move money, so ordinary customer service is inseparable from authorization, fraud detection, and policy compliance. Existing financial-fraud benchmarks classify static transactions or messages, and general agent-safety benchmarks target prompt injection or generic harmful use; none test whether a policy-grounded banking agent safely acts when a caller manipulates identity, authorization, and trust over a conversation. We introduce FraudBench, an executable benchmark built on the $τ^2$-bench dual-control framework and the $τ$-Knowledge banking environment. Both the agent and the simulated caller act through tools over shared, mutable account state, and the agent may grant the caller access to selected tools; the environment exposes a 698-document internal policy corpus that the agent must retrieve from. FraudBench contains 150 authored adversarial scenarios; a frozen public set of 107 (90 across ten fraud mechanisms plus 17 chained adaptive attacks) is used for all reported runs, with 43 further chained attacks held out. Safety is history-dependent: single-control tasks satisfy every precondition but one, and adaptive attacks make a later, locally valid request unsafe because of an earlier probe, admission, or failed attempt. Each scenario is annotated with observable evidence, prohibited actions, safe dispositions, and intervention points. A preliminary single-trial evaluation of four agents on the 107 graded tasks yields attack-security between 49\% and 65\%, with money-mule and first-party fraud the most common cross-model weaknesses.

</details>


### [123] [ComponentBench: Diagnosing Component-Level Failures in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18307)
*Tianchen Guan,Xinlei Lin,Royce Cheng-Yue,Xiangjun Wang,Shuyan Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ComponentBench，一个面向计算机使用智能体的组件级评估基准，包含97个标准UI组件的2,910个程序化验证任务，并配备人类参考轨迹以评估任务成功率和交互效率。通过评估7个模型在4种观察与动作空间上的表现，发现设计选择对性能有重大影响，同一模型在不同空间上的成功率差异超过30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体评估在长周期工作流基准和原子级GUI接地测试之间存在空白层：缺少对现实组件中心交互（如切换按钮组）的评估。这一中间层交互足够短以便诊断，又足够丰富以捕捉现代界面的负担，但目前缺乏系统性的评测工具。

Method: 构建ComponentBench基准：围绕97个标准UI组件的本体论组织，实例化为2,910个跨主流组件库的程序化验证任务；搭配清洗后的人类参考轨迹以评估任务成功率和交互效率；引入可扩展的审计管线，用于评估实现后的结构化难度并综合跨任务和组件族的失败分析。

Result: 评估了7个模型（GPT-5.4、Gemini 3 Flash、GPT-5.4 mini、GPT-5 mini、Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite、Qwen3-VL-235B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B）在4种观察与动作空间上的表现。同一模型仅改变观察和动作空间，任务成功率可变化超过30%（如GPT-5 mini从83.1%的可访问性树观察降至48.9%的纯坐标像素控制）。最快配置耗时仍为匹配人类参考的3.7倍，且对人类而言简单的空间操作仍对当前智能体构成挑战。

Conclusion: 组件级评估揭示了观察与动作空间选择对智能体性能的关键影响，以及当前智能体在交互效率和空间操作方面与人类仍存在显著差距。ComponentBench为计算机使用智能体的系统化诊断和改进提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Current evaluation of computer-use agents is split between long-horizon workflow benchmarks and atomic GUI-grounding tests. This leaves an under-instrumented middle layer: realistic component-centered interactions (e.g., toggle a button set) that are short enough to diagnose and rich enough to capture the burdens of modern interfaces. We present ComponentBench, a benchmark and diagnostic pipeline for component-level evaluation of computer-use agents on modern web UIs. ComponentBench is organized around a library-agnostic ontology of 97 canonical UI components instantiated as 2,910 programmatically verified tasks across widely used component libraries, paired with cleaned human reference trajectories that enable evaluation of both task success and interaction efficiency. Beyond task collection, we introduce a scalable pipeline for auditing realized structural difficulty after implementation and synthesizing structured failure analyses across tasks and component families. Evaluating seven models -- GPT-5.4, Gemini 3 Flash, GPT-5.4 mini, GPT-5 mini, Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite, Qwen3-VL-235B, and UI-TARS-1.5-7B -- across four observation and action spaces, we show that these design choices critically impact performance. Within a single shared harness, changing only the observation and action space shifts task success by more than 30% for the same model: GPT-5 mini falls from 83.1% with accessibility-tree observations to 48.9% with coordinate-only Pixel control. Moreover, even the fastest configuration takes 3.7x as long as the matched human reference, and spatial manipulations that are trivial for humans continue to challenge current agents.

</details>


### [124] [Efficient Adaptation of LLMs for Hate Speech Detection in Low-Resource Languages: A Comparative Study on Roman Urdu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18142)
*Toneema Zubair,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Faisal Kamiran,Hafiz Hassan Saeed,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对罗马乌尔都语（Roman Urdu）这一低资源语言，使用LoRA参数高效微调方法对多种大型语言模型进行仇恨言论检测，在包含72,000+标注评论的PURUTT数据集上取得F1>0.93的优异性能，远超零样本推理（F1=0.56）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（如罗马乌尔都语）缺乏标注数据、语言结构非正式且拼写不规范，使得仇恨言论检测极具挑战性。作者旨在系统评估LLMs在该场景下的检测能力，并探索参数高效微调方法的适用性。

Method: 采用PEFT中的LoRA方法对Mistral、LLaMA、Falcon及多语言BERT等不同Transformer模型进行微调，并与零样本推理进行对比基准测试。实验使用PURUTT数据集（包含72,000余条标注评论）。

Result: 零样本模型表现中等（F1=0.56），而仅更新少量可训练参数即可显著提升分类性能（F1>0.93）。PEFT在计算效率和性能上均表现卓越。

Conclusion: LoRA参数高效微调方法在低资源语言处理任务中展现出出色的性能与计算效率，是处理此类任务的理想选择。

Abstract: It is challenging to detect hate speech in Low Resource Languages (LRLs) because of the absence of annotated data, the informality of its language structure, and the lack of standardized grammar. A good example of such a challenge is Roman Urdu which is broadly used by South Asians on social media and has a high variation while lacking contextually consistent spellings. The objective of this paper is to conduct a comprehensive assessment of Large Language Models (LLMs) for Hate Speech Detection (HSD) in Roman Urdu script and fine-tune these models using the Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method called Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA). To evaluate zero-shot inference, we benchmarked it against PEFT on different transformer models, including Mistral, LLaMA, Falcon, and multilingual BERT. Experiments are conducted on the PURUTT (Parallel Urdu and Roman Urdu Corpus for Toxic Comments and Transliteration) dataset with over 72,000 annotated comments. The results suggest that zero shot models perform moderately (F1 = 0.56), but updating a small fraction of the model trainable parameters improves the classification performance significantly (F1 > 0.93). Our results have shown that PEFT delivers outstanding performance alongside excellent computational efficiency, making it highly suitable for low-resource language processing tasks.

</details>


### [125] [RDFdL: Integrating RDF with Differential Dynamic Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18165)
*Yuyang Li,Lukas Kubelka,Julia Butte,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RDFdL框架，将RDF知识图谱与微分动态逻辑(dL)集成，使RDF不仅能够描述静态知识，还能表示和推理物理系统的连续动态行为（如微分方程描述的系統），并通过一阶逻辑的共享基础实现动态逻辑验证结果与SPARQL查询的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDF建模的知识图谱擅长描述静态知识，但无法捕获或推理物理系统的动态行为（如微分方程描述的系统），这是AI驱动的信息物理系统（CPS）中的关键缺口。

Method: 提出RDFdL框架：在动态部分，用RDF和SHACL语法表示微分方程及状态空间范围，并通过到dL的翻译提供语义；利用RDF与dL在一阶逻辑上的共同基础实现集成，使安全性和可达性验证结果可作为蕴涵用于RDF数据上的SPARQL查询；使用Apache Jena进行本体驱动的RDF推理，使用KeYmaera X作为dL定理证明器实现流水线。

Result: 实现了RDFdL流水线，将RDF推理与dL定理证明结合，验证结果（安全性和可达性）可作为蕴涵提供给SPARQL查询，并在制造业场景中展示了其应用可行性。

Conclusion: RDFdL弥合了语义网与动态系统验证之间的鸿沟，使得基于RDF的知识表示能够无缝支持对物理系统动态行为的推理，为AI驱动的信息物理系统提供统一的知识与验证框架。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs modeled in RDF are powerful for describing static knowledge, but they cannot capture or reason about the dynamic behavior of physical systems, e.g., systems described by differential equations, which is a critical gap for AI-driven cyber-physical systems. To solve this, we propose RDFdL, a framework that integrates RDF with Differential Dynamic Logic (dL) to represent and reason about both static knowledge and the continuous dynamics of physical systems. For the dynamic part, we syntactically represent differential equations and ranges in the state space in RDF and SHACL and provide semantics using a translation to dL. Linking RDF and dL through their shared foundation in first-order logic achieves a unique integration: verification results for safety and reachability properties in the dynamic logic domain become available as entailment to SPARQL queries over RDF data. We implement the pipeline using Apache Jena for ontology-driven RDF reasoning and KeYmaera X, the theorem prover for dL, and sketch its applicability in manufacturing.

</details>


### [126] [Adversarial Review: Structured Disagreement for Grounded Agentic Code Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18167)
*Eric S. Qiu,Joyce Gill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Adversarial Review (AR)，一种极简协作式代码审查协议，主编码智能体配合审阅者与批评者两个智能体，通过结构化分歧审查实现高效协作，在多个基准上超越五智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期多智能体LLM系统通过角色分离团队扩展智能体数量但收益递减，而将智能体视为被动工具的子智能体范式又完全失去了智能体交互的优势，因此需要探索一条中间路线：实现最小程度的智能体协作，同时避免大型多智能体团队的额外开销。

Method: 提出Adversarial Review (AR)协议，由主编码智能体、审阅者智能体和批评者智能体三者协作：审阅者评估代码质量，批评者通过结构化分歧对审阅结果进行审查，最后由主智能体根据反馈进行代码修改。该协议强调分歧的最小化、结构化和证据支撑。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench上取得最高通过率，仅用三智能体超越五智能体基线；在SWE-PRBench上，朴素AR暴露了虚假共识失败模式，通过单次提示迭代显式添加分歧后取得最高F1；在SWE-bench Verified上同样优于基线。

Conclusion: 协作式代码审查并不需要大量智能体或复杂的通信结构，关键在于分歧应当是极简的、结构化的且有证据支撑的。AR验证了这一核心观点，为多智能体协作范式提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Early multi-agent LLM systems often used role-separated teams, yet scaling agent count yields diminishing returns on repository-level coding tasks. Recent alternatives treat agents as passive tools (subagents), yet this removes the benefits of agent interaction entirely. We study whether a subagent paradigm can support a middle ground: minimal agentic cooperation without the overhead of large multi-agent teams. We introduce Adversarial Review (AR), a minimal cooperative code-review protocol in which a main coding agent works with a reviewer and a critic agent. The reviewer evaluates code, while the critic audits the review through structured disagreement before the main agent edits. On LiveCodeBench, AR achieves the highest pass rate among tested methods, outperforming a five-agent baseline while using only three agents. On SWE-PRBench, naive AR exposes a false-consensus failure mode, where agents converge on agreement without sufficient evidence, but a single prompt iteration that adds disagreement explicitly achieves the highest F1 among tested methods. On SWE-bench Verified, AR also shows improvements over the baselines on repository-level coding tasks. Together, AR demonstrates that cooperative code review does not require many agents or complex communication structures: it requires that disagreement be minimal, structured, and evidence-grounded.

</details>


### [127] [Looped Language Models Improve Compositional Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18171)
*Andrei Cristian Popescu,Haitz Sáez de Ocáriz Borde,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了循环语言模型（Looped Language Models）在智能体工具调用场景中的潜力，通过在组合式工具调用设置下评估原生和改造的循环模型，发现循环计算对依赖感知的组合工具使用有积极效果，且自适应推理能在计算-性能权衡上取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型在推理基准上表现出色，但其在智能体工具使用（agentic tool use）方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。作者希望填补这一空白，系统研究循环架构在需要多步骤API调用、状态维护和依赖管理场景中的表现。

Method: 在API-Bank、BFCL和NESTful三个基准上，对原生循环语言模型和改造的循环语言模型进行评估。具体做法是：1) 对比循环与非循环模型在匹配的监督微调配方下的表现；2) 在推理时改变循环深度；3) 评估自适应推理策略在计算分配上的效果。

Result: 1) 循环计算在组合式和依赖感知的工具使用场景中普遍带来性能提升；2) 对于孤立的API调用场景，循环带来的增益较小且更多依赖具体模型；3) 多步工具使用的准确率通常随循环深度增加而提升；4) 自适应推理通过仅在需要时分配额外计算，实现了更优的计算-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 循环语言模型是构建需要可靠规划、协调和执行组合工具工作流的智能体系统的有前景的架构选择。

Abstract: Looped language models have shown promising results on reasoning benchmarks, yet their potential for agentic tool use remains largely unexplored. We study this question in compositional tool-calling settings, where models must coordinate multiple API calls, maintain intermediate state, and preserve dependencies across tool interactions. We evaluate native and retrofitted looped language models on API-Bank, BFCL, and NESTful, comparing looped and non-looped models trained under matched supervised fine-tuning recipes and varying recurrent depth at inference time. In controlled experiments, recurrent computation generally benefits compositional and dependency-aware tool use, while providing smaller and more model-dependent gains on isolated API invocation. Accuracy on multi-step tool use generally increases with recurrent depth; adaptive inference, however, achieves a more favorable compute-performance trade-off by allocating additional computation only when needed. Our results suggest that looped language models are a promising architecture for agentic systems that require reliable planning, coordination, and execution of compositional tool use workflows.

</details>


### [128] [On the Triangle Inequality for the Jaccard Distance in Arbitrary Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18194)
*Costin Bădică,Amelia Bădică*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了关于Jaccard距离在格和实值赋值上推广的新理论结果，证明了在不同格结构条件下三角不等式成立的必要与充分条件，并将这些结果与量子信息论、形式概念分析和机器学习等计算领域联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Jaccard距离到更一般的格结构上，突破了以往结果依赖于分配性的局限，探索在更宽松的结构条件下（如任意格、相对补分配格、节补分配格）Jaccard距离何时能保持度量性质。

Method: 通过在不同的格结构（任意格、相对补分配格、节补分配格）上，结合不同性质的实值赋值（严格正、单调、模、超模、对数子模等），系统地证明或证伪Jaccard距离满足三角不等式等度量性质。

Result: （1）当赋值严格正、单调且模时，任意格上Jaccard距离满足三角不等式；（2）在相对补分配格上，正、单调、超模且对数子模的赋值条件下三角不等式成立；（3）将对称差Jaccard公式推广到节补分配格上的子模赋值；（4）证明超模性是标准广义Jaccard距离成为有效度量的必要条件；（5）讨论了放松结构约束在实际计算领域的应用价值。

Conclusion: 本研究系统地刻画了Jaccard距离在各类格结构上成为有效度量的充分与必要条件，放宽了传统上对分配性和全局界的要求，为Jaccard距离在更广泛的数学结构与实际计算场景中的应用奠定了理论基础，并指出了若干待解决的开放数学问题。

Abstract: This paper presents new theoretical results on generalizing the Jaccard distance for lattices and real valuations. We demonstrate that when the valuation is strictly positive, monotone, and modular, the Jaccard distance satisfies the triangle inequality on arbitrary lattices, effectively generalizing earlier results that depended heavily on distributivity. Moving to relatively complemented distributive lattices (which safely drop the requirement for the global bounds found in Boolean algebras), we prove the triangle inequality holds as long as the valuation is positive, monotone, supermodular, and $\log$-submodular. Additionally, we adapt the symmetric-difference Jaccard formulation for submodular valuations to sectionally complemented distributive lattices. Shifting to necessary conditions, we prove that supermodularity is a strict requirement for the standard generalized Jaccard distance to operate as a valid metric. Finally, we map the practical value of relaxing these structural constraints to computational fields like quantum information theory, formal concept analysis, and machine learning, closing with a brief look at open mathematical problems.

</details>


### [129] [GenEx: A Graph-Based Representational Paradigm for SARS-CoV-2 Variant Detection via Codon Co-occurrence Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18238)
*Arefin Amin,Labiba Faiza Karim,M. Monir Uddin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenEx管道，将SARS-CoV-2等病毒的基因序列转换为密码子共现图，提取超过25个图特征，并利用SVD谱图特征提取（使用平方奇异值而非传统特征值）增强类别可分性，在23个基准ML模型上实现了对Beta、Gamma、Delta和Omicron等变异株的高精度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的病毒基因组分析方法（如序列比对、系统发育分析和突变频率统计）将基因序列视为线性字符串，使用成对距离矩阵，无法捕捉序列中复杂的上下文相互依赖性，因此需要一种能更好表征基因序列结构信息的表示方法。

Method: 提出GenEx管道，核心包括两种图生成算法MSCG（多尺度密码子共现图）和LAPCG（线性时间邻接PMI密码子图），将密码子序列转化为结构化符号词汇表，构建密码子共现图；随后提取超过25个图特征，并使用基于SVD的谱图特征提取，采用平方奇异值（σ²）替代传统特征值以放大主次谱分量的分离度。

Result: 在23个基准ML模型上针对最新的SARS-CoV-2变异株进行训练，在所有SARS-CoV-2变异株检测中取得了显著成果。

Conclusion: GenEx证明将计算语言学的图表示范式引入病毒基因组分析是有效的，能够显著提升变异株分类检测的性能，为基因组学分析提供了新的方法论视角。

Abstract: Genomic analysis on viruses such as SARS-CoV-2 variants: Beta, Gamma, Delta, and Omicron is heavily dominated by classical bioinformatics methods, including Sequence Alignment, Phylogenetic Analysis, and Mutation Frequency Statistics. These approaches use pairwise codon or nucleotide distance matrices to analyze gene sequences, treating them as linear strings rather than capturing their complex contextual interdependencies. We proposed GenEx, a pipeline that converts raw gene sequences into codon co-occurrence graphs and extracts more than 25 graph features. Our two most prominent techniques for graph generation and feature extraction are MSCG (Multi-Scale Codon Co-occurrence Graph) and LAPCG (Linear-time Adjacency PMI Codon Graph). Using these algorithms, we treated codon sequences as structured symbolic vocabularies interpretable to codon co-occurrence graph analysis, a representational paradigm borrowed from computational linguistics. Another major contribution includes implementing a spectral graph feature extraction using Singular Value Decomposition (SVD), using the squared singular value ($σ^2$) instead of the traditionally used eigenvalue, which helped us to amplify the separation between dominant and subdominant spectral components, thereby enhancing inter-class separability in downstream classification. And to further demonstrate that our method works, we trained 23 benchmarked ML models against the latest SARS-CoV-2 variants, achieving remarkable results in detecting all SARS-CoV-2 variants.

</details>


### [130] [Redakto - The Incognito Tab for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18260)
*Saurav Kumar Saha,Tom Röhr,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Redakto，一个开源文本匿名化工具，可在将文本输入LLM之前去除或伪匿名化个人身份信息（PII）。该工具提供Web应用、REST API和MCP钩子等多种使用方式，在法律和医疗领域的实证评估中，匿名化文本在实用性上与原文本相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在日常应用中的普及带来了隐私挑战，尤其是EU新法规（如GDPR）对个人身份信息处理提出了严格要求。隐私不确定性可能阻碍创新速度和研究向应用的转化，亟需有效的文本匿名化解决方案。

Method: Redakto工具提供去除（redaction）和伪匿名化（pseudonymization）两种功能，通过Web应用、REST API和模型上下文协议（MCP）钩子供不同用户群体使用。系统完全开源、计算资源需求低，可部署在本地硬件上。作者在法律和医疗领域文本上进行了广泛的实证评估，同时衡量隐私性和实用性。

Result: 在多种去除策略下，匿名化文本的实用性得分与原文本相当，表明Redakto的匿名化处理不会对LLM任务产生显著的负面影响，在隐私保护和文本可用性之间取得了良好的平衡。

Conclusion: Redakto提供了一种实用、开放且易于部署的文本匿名化方案，能够有效解决LLM使用中的隐私合规问题，同时保持匿名化后文本在LLM任务中的实用性，具有从研究到应用转化的潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being increasingly used in everyday applications. A major challenge in the context of LLMs or Artificial Intelligence (AI) in general is to ensure privacy when using them, meaning that personally identifiable information (PII) is removed from any text that enters an LLM. These challenges have become more urgent with novel EU legislation. Uncertainty around LLM usage with respect to privacy concerns in EU countries can be a major blocker for the speed of innovation and transfer from research to applications. Here we present \textbf{Redakto}, a tool that can be used for anonymizing text prior to feeding it to an LLM or other downstream text processing. We provide state-of-the-art functionalities for both redaction of PII but also when used for pseudonymization. These functionalities are exposed such that they can easily be used by end-users, through the Redakto web application, and by developers and researchers, via REST APIs and model context protocol (MCP) hooks. The implementation is fully open source, requires modest compute resources, and can be readily deployed on local hardware. In contrast to prior work and in order to better assess the quality of the anonymized texts, we conduct extensive empirical evaluations on textual data from legal and medical domain with respect to both privacy and utility of the redacted texts. Our empirical results demonstrate that the texts anonymized with different redaction strategies achieve utility scores on par with the original texts, suggesting that anonymization with Redakto can be used for LLM tasks without substantial negative impact for the tasks we explored.

</details>


### [131] [Cacheable by Design? Training Mixture-of-Experts Routers for Locality Against the Edge Memory-Bandwidth Wall: A Pre-Registered Negative Result with a Systems Measurement Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18261)
*Shriniwas Ramesh Suram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在单张8GB GPU上服务235B参数的MoE模型时遇到的内存带宽瓶颈，并构建了llama-moe-trace工具量化路由局部性。作者预注册了带辅助局部性损失和领域路由损失的137M MoE模型训练实验，发现缓存命中下降（最高减少60%）与困惑度质量（≤1%阈值）存在紧密耦合，所有配置均未通过质量门槛。训练无关的缓存感知重路由与训练局部性结合可达到~80%的缺失率降低且困惑度损失控制在3.4%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级GPU上服务大型MoE模型受限于内存带宽而非算力，绝大多数专家权重存储在远慢于RAM的SSD上，导致解码速度极慢。现有研究缺乏严格的、预注册的、多领域的评估来验证专家缓存的"可训练性"是否真正可行且不影响模型质量。

Method: 通过量化Qwen3-235B的带宽墙模型和批处理摊销失败，构建零侵入的路由遥测工具llama-moe-trace；预注册137M MoE模型训练实验，引入辅助局部性损失和领域路由损失，并设定缓存缺失降低+困惑度≤1%的联合标准；同时在340M规模上验证尺度效应，并测试训练无关的缓存感知重路由与领域预取的效果。

Result: Qwen3-235B实测解码0.44 tok/s（热缓存），批处理batch 32时因分页抖动崩溃；路由显示相邻token专家重用为随机概率2.0倍，13.4%专家的LRU缓存可服务66%请求。训练辅助损失可将缓存缺失降低最多60%（99%静态固定命中率），但所有配置均未通过≤1%困惑度门槛；340M规模下质量税略有上升而非下降。训练无关重路由+训练局部性结合达到~80%缺失降低且困惑度≤3.4%，成本远低于单独方案；领域预取无效。

Conclusion: MoE模型的专家缓存可训练性在多领域、预注册的严格评估下并不"免费"——缓存缺失减少与模型质量之间存在根本性的紧密耦合。训练无关的缓存感知重路由可以作为训练局部性损失的强力补充，二者结合是降低缓存缺失更经济有效的路径。研究者开源了全部代码、轨迹数据和预注册方案。

Abstract: Serving a 235B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model on a single 8 GB GPU is bottlenecked not by compute but by memory bandwidth: decode must stream each token's active experts from whichever tier holds them, and on consumer hardware most experts sit on an SSD far slower than RAM. We quantify this bandwidth wall on Qwen3-235B (Q4_K_M, 134 GB): measured decode is 0.44 tok/s warm, matching a bytes-per-token / bandwidth model, while a batching scheme that should amortize one disk sweep instead collapses at batch 32 from paging thrash. We build llama-moe-trace, a zero-surgery router-telemetry tool, and measure routing on Qwen3-30B: adjacent-token expert reuse is 2.0x chance, 95% of traffic uses 52.5% of experts, and an LRU cache of 13.4% of experts serves 66% of requests. We then ask whether cacheability is trainable: we pre-register training of 137M MoE language models with auxiliary locality and domain router losses, under joint criteria on cache-miss reduction and perplexity. The mechanism works (misses down up to 60%; a 99% static-pin hit rate) but every configuration fails the pre-registered <=1% perplexity gate -- miss reduction and quality are tightly coupled. Concurrent StickyMoE reports the same loss as near-free on single-domain sub-25M models; on multi-domain 137M we find the tax real. Our contribution is this pre-registered, stricter-criterion, multi-domain evaluation plus edge-serving measurements. A 340M rung shows the tax does not shrink with scale (it rises slightly). We further show training-free cache-aware rerouting stacks with trained locality -- together ~80% miss reduction at <=3.4% perplexity at both sizes, far cheaper than either alone -- while domain-primed prefetching does not help. All code, traces, and the pre-registration are released.

</details>


### [132] [Evaluating Structured Information Extraction with Open Models in a High Risk Public Sector Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18289)
*Elias Schubert,Felix Bießmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对开源文档信息提取系统在高风险场景下端到端性能的综合基准测试，基于国际学生申请处理任务，评估了OCR引擎、LLM和VLM的组合系统。结果显示VLM普遍优于OCR+LLM流水线，但即使最先进的零样本模型也难堪此任，仅4/35配置的F1超过0.5，约75%的配置低于0.25。模型规模与性能呈非线性关系，OCR输出的结构保真度是独立于下游模型能力的关键影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管开源OCR、LLM和VLM生态系统快速发展，但缺乏对现实多步骤信息提取流水线的系统评估。尤其在欧盟AI法案将公共部门应用归类为高风险的情况下，负责任地使用这些工具需要基于现实任务进行全面评估，当前研究空白亟待填补。

Method: 构建了一个端到端基准测试，选取国际学习项目学生申请这一高风险文档处理任务，对多种先进的开源OCR引擎、LLM和VLM进行组合配置，在零样本设置下评估各配置的提取性能，共测试了35种配置组合。

Result: VLM整体优于OCR+LLM流水线；最先进的OCR+LLM流水线可与顶级VLM性能媲美，但大多数OCR+LLM组合表现明显逊色。仅4/35配置F1超过0.5，约75%配置低于0.25。模型规模与性能呈非线性关系，大幅增加模型规模并不能保证成比例的性能提升；OCR输出结构保真度是独立于下游能力的决定性因素。

Conclusion: 开源模型在处理高风险现实文档提取任务时仍不够可靠，VLM是更有前景的方向，但整体上开源方案在零样本端到端场景下性能不足。提升OCR输出质量（特别是结构保真度）比单纯增大模型规模更关键，未来需要在结构化输出保证和流水线优化方面持续改进。

Abstract: The extraction of structured information from unstructured documents represents a critical component of digital transformations in all sectors. While proprietary solutions dominate commercial applications, a rapidly growing ecosystem of open-source Optical Character Recognition (OCR) engines, Large Language Models (LLMs), and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offers accessible alternatives. However, systematic evaluations on realistic, multi-step extraction pipelines remain scarce. Responsible usage of such extraction tools require comprehensive evaluations on realistic tasks, especially as these solutions will be key components of applications in the public sector that the EU AI act categorizes as high risk. To address this gap we present a comprehensive benchmark assessing the end-to-end performance of open-source systems on a complex real-world document processing task classified as high risk: Student applications for an international study program. We conduct a comprehensive empirical evaluation with state-of-the-art OCR engines, LLMs and VLMs. Our results reveal that while VLMs generally outperform OCR+LLM pipelines, even state-of-the-art open-source models struggle to handle such tasks reliably in zero-shot settings. Only 4 of 35 configurations achieved F1 scores above 0.5, with the best OCR+LLM pipeline matching top VLM performance, though most OCR+LLM combinations performed substantially worse. Roughly 75\% of all configurations scored below 0.25. Model scale influences performance, yet the relationship is non-linear: substantially larger models do not guarantee proportionally better results. Input quality, particularly the structural preservation of OCR output, emerges as a critical factor independent of downstream model capability.

</details>


### [133] [The Lifecycle of LLM-as-a-Judge for Large-Scale Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18300)
*Emma Yanyang Kong,JJ Tan,Ishan Gupta,Lars Olds,Claire Campbell,David Fagnan,Veli Balin,Rohan Gosain,Louis Garcia,Minsu Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM评判器的全生命周期框架，涵盖构建、训练、部署和持续监控四个阶段，并在Netflix大规模推荐解释系统中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-a-Judge研究大多将评判器视为静态工件，只在其构建时或针对固定基准进行一次评估，忽视了生产系统中评判器需要随数据演变而持续维护的现实需求。

Method: 提出四阶段生命周期框架：(I)构建阶段定义多种评估标准并构建带人工标注和推理依据的基准数据集；(II)训练阶段采用推理对齐的规则微调(RART)方法，利用元评判器的推理输出作为学习信号优化规则；(III)部署阶段一个评判器同时承担质量门控和反思生成两种生产角色；(IV)监控阶段通过人在回路对齐流程检测漂移并触发重新训练。

Result: 在Netflix推荐解释场景中，评判器每周评估数十万条剧集级解释，服务数百万会员。五周A/B测试表明，与无解释对照组相比，评判器对齐的解释使会员观看转向新内容并增加了成功浏览到播放的会话，且无质量相关的下架事件。

Conclusion: LLM评判器在生产系统中应被视为拥有生命周期的动态工件，而非一次性构建的静态工具；所提出的四阶段框架和RART方法在实际大规模工业场景中有效可行。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge, which leverages a large language model to evaluate natural language generated by another AI application or model, has become a standard, scalable approach for accelerating and extending costly human evaluation. However, most work treats a judge as a static artifact, evaluating it once at construction or against a fixed benchmark. In contrast, we argue that an LLM judge running in a production system is better understood as having a lifecycle: it must be built, trained, deployed, and continuously maintained as the surrounding data evolves, and each phase poses distinct technical and operational challenges.
  We present such a lifecycle for the LLM judges that evaluate user-facing recommendation explanations at Netflix, where our pipeline generates and the judges assess hundreds of thousands of distinct show-level explanations per week, served across the mobile experience to millions of members. Our framework has four phases: (I) Birth, defining multiple evaluation criteria and building curated benchmark datasets with human labels and rationales; (II) Training, refining the judges' rubrics via Reasoning-Aligned Rubric Tuning (RART), a rubric-tuning procedure that uses a meta-judge over reasoning output as the learning signal; (III) Deployment, in which one judge serves two production roles: quality gating and reflective generation; and (IV) Monitoring, a continuous Human-in-the-Loop alignment process that detects drift and triggers re-tuning behind a human review gate. We report post-launch results from a five-week A/B test over tens of millions of members, in which the judge-aligned explanations shifted member viewing toward novel content (previously unwatched) and increased successful browse-to-play sessions relative to a no-explanation control, with no quality-related takedowns.

</details>


### [134] [SESSE: Sketch, Expand, Sort, Summarize, Evaluate -- LLM-as-Judge Evaluation via Structured Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18303)
*Dae Lee,Mihai Delgeanu,Adel Youssef*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SESSE（Sketch, Expand, Sort, Summarize, Evaluate）框架，将LLM-as-judge的整体性A/B偏好判断分解为结构化子问题，无需训练即可实现与微调专家模型相当的性能，同时提供可解释的审计轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM-as-judge方法将响应质量评估简化为单一的整体A/B偏好选择，无法隔离驱动偏好的具体质量维度，也难以区分模型错误与真实标签模糊性。

Method: SESSE框架包含五个步骤：Sketch（草拟）、Expand（扩展）、Sort（排序）、Summarize（总结）、Evaluate（评估）。核心思想是从评判模型自身的错误案例中挖掘结构化子问题，将整体判断分解为多个子标准进行评估，无需oracle响应、任务特定评分标准或微调。

Result: 在RewardBench（n=1,000）上，SESSE与思维链基线表现接近，并与微调专家模型RISE-Judge-32B（92.7%）竞争力相当，同时完全无需训练。每个标准的投票证据提供了可解释的审计轨迹，可用于诊断标签模糊性和评判模型失败模式。

Conclusion: SESSE通过分解整体判断为结构化子问题，不仅保持了与微调专家模型相当的性能，还提供了从单一整体输出标记中无法获得的细粒度可解释性，有助于识别标签模糊性和模型失败模式。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation reduces response quality assessment to a single holistic A/B preference choice, providing no mechanism to isolate which quality dimensions drove the preference or distinguish model errors from genuine label ambiguity. We propose SESSE (Sketch, Expand, Sort, Summarize, Evaluate), a training-free framework that decomposes holistic judgment into structured sub-questions mined directly from the judge's own error cases; requiring no oracle responses, task-specific rubrics, or fine-tuning. On RewardBench (n=1,000), SESSE achieves near-parity with the chain-of-thought baseline and is competitive with RISE-Judge-32B (92.7%), a fine-tuned specialist, while remaining fully training-free. Per-criterion vote evidence provides an interpretable audit trail for diagnosing label ambiguity and judge failure modes unavailable from a single holistic output token.

</details>


### [135] [Governance Records as Supervision: Verifier-Selected Self-Training for Structured Workflow Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18324)
*Jesus Salas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究机器可验证的工作流记录是否能用于监督有界模型，将稀缺或昂贵的验证能力整合为可靠的一次性执行。实验表明，利用VAL验证器接受的计划训练Qwen3-14B，使接受计划数从1提升到57（验证器选择优于模型自选等其他选取方式），验证器选择的监督对机器可检查的有界能力有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器可验证的工作流会产生治理记录，问题在于这些记录能否用于监督有界模型，将偶发或昂贵的能力整合为可靠的一次性执行，从而无需Oracle目标或更强大的教师模型。

Method: 利用PlanBench重规划案例中由独立VAL验证器接受的计划（24个），训练同一checkpoint进行非思考执行；进行匹配消融实验（固定源案例、候选池、目标数、模型、配方和种子），比较base、schema选择、模型自选和VAL选择四种目标选取方式；并补充Phi更强教师实验。

Result: 在80个未开放案例中，VAL接受计划从1增至57（56个配对增益，零回归），思考模式仅达30；匹配消融在160个新案例中，base/schema/自选/VAL选择分别达到1/55/69/102，VAL比自选净增+33（p=0.0000019647）；Phi加强教师臂从2提升至51个接受计划，schema有效输出从35提升至80。

Conclusion: 验证器选择的监督对有界、机器可检查的能力有效（而非任意规划、企业有效性或不受限的自我改进）；独立语义选择相对于匹配备选方案是承重的关键因素；接口治愈门未通过，但整体结果支持将验证器选择用于有界能力的监督学习。

Abstract: Machine-verifiable workflows produce governance records linking a task contract, model attempt, verifier decision, accepted output, and target origin. We test whether these records can supervise bounded models, consolidating occasional or expensive capability into reliable one-shot execution.
  On fresh, structure-disjoint PlanBench replanning cases, Qwen3-14B thinking generated 24 plans admitted by the independently authored VAL verifier. Those plans trained the same checkpoint for non-thinking execution, without oracle targets or a stronger teacher. On 80 unopened cases, VAL-accepted plans increased from 1 to 57, with 56 paired gains and zero regressions; thinking reached 30. The adapter was schema-valid on all cases and used approximately 1/56 of thinking's mean latency. The separate paired interface-cure gate did not pass.
  A matched ablation fixed the source cases, 52-candidate pool, 24-target count, model, recipe, and seed while changing target selection. On 160 new cases, base, schema-selected, model-self-selected, and VAL-selected execution reached 1, 55, 69, and 102 accepted plans. VAL exceeded self-selection by paired net +33 (p=0.0000019647), with gains in both difficulty strata. Independent semantic selection is therefore load-bearing relative to matched alternatives within this band.
  A complementary Phi stronger-teacher arm raised base Phi-4 from 2 to 51 accepted plans and from 35 to 80 schema-valid outputs. Earlier synthetic experiments establish teachability, cumulative learning, construction robustness, and stopping boundaries. The results support verifier-selected supervision for bounded, machine-checkable capabilities, not arbitrary planning, enterprise validity, or unrestricted self-improvement.

</details>


### [136] [Measuring the Partial-Credit Gap: A Strict Benchmark on Vietnam's 2025 Convex Marking Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18336)
*Nguyen Quoc Hung,Nguyen Dang Minh,Le Nhu Quynh,Tran Khanh Linh,Nguyen Kieu Linh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，用"正确/错误"二分类和平均准确率来评估语言模型在人类考试上的表现，忽视了非可加性评分规则下部分知识不应获得比例学分的问题。以2025年越南高考改革为案例，提出THPT-Ladder基准（21份官方试卷、632题、11个科目），按官方凸性评分规则（0/0.10/0.25/0.50/1.00）评估模型，发现标准准确率指标会高估模型能力，且误差分布方式会影响最终成绩，甚至改变模型在人类考生中的百分位排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准用准确率假设部分知识值得按比例计分，但现实中考试可能采用非可加性评分方案，导致模型能力评估失真。作者以越南2025年高考改革为例，说明这种替换会造成显著评分偏差，因此需要建立更贴近真实考试规则的评估基准。

Method: 作者构建了THPT-Ladder基准，包含21份官方考试的632道题目，覆盖11个科目，完全按照越南教育部官方凸性评分规则（0、0.10、0.25、0.50、1.00分）为模型打分。利用教育部公开的超过百万考生的成绩数据，将模型直接放入人类考生分布中进行百分位排名比较。评估了8个模型，并分析了准确性水平相同但错误分布不同的情况对最终分数的影响。

Result: 官方评分规则下，每个Part II问题比比例学分少0.020至0.159分。例如Qwen3.5-27B在2025年历史考试中因0.042分差距从人类考生的第90百分位降至第77百分位（在481,293名考生中）。模型准确率无法预测这种惩罚——在Claude Sonnet 5的准确率水平下，不同错误分布导致每问得分在0.869到0.932分之间波动。

Conclusion: 标准基准报告的准确率反映的是一种机构不会认可的"能力"，因为官方成绩取决于正确陈述如何分组而非简单计数。评估语言模型时应采用与真实考试完全一致的评分规则，否则会系统性高估模型在教育场景中的实际表现。

Abstract: When evaluating language models on human exams, benchmarks typically score each response as right or wrong and report the overall accuracy. This approach assumes that partial knowledge is worth proportional credit, an assumption that fails when an examination uses a non-additive grading scheme. The 2025 reform of Vietnam's National High School Graduation Examination demonstrates the cost of this substitution. In Part II of the exam, candidates evaluate four true/false statements per question. The grading is convex: the number of correct statements earns 0, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, or 1.00 points. Identifying three statements correctly pays 0.50 points, not the 0.75 points that standard accuracy metrics would award. Because Part II accounts for 4.00 of the exam's 10.00 points, reporting accuracy inflates the score by rewarding partial knowledge that the state explicitly penalizes. We introduce THPT-Ladder, a benchmark of 632 items from 21 official exams across 11 subjects, graded exactly as the ministry grades its students. The ministry publishes the marks of over a million candidates, allowing us to place models directly into the human cohort. Across eight models, the official rubric pays 0.020 to 0.159 points less per Part II question than proportional credit. This shortfall changes a model's apparent competence. For Qwen3.5-27B on the 2025 History exam, a 0.042-point shortfall drops its standing from the 90th to the 77th percentile among 481,293 candidates. A model's accuracy does not predict this penalty. At Claude Sonnet 5's accuracy level, different distributions of errors yield scores varying from 0.869 to 0.932 points per question.
